Mandating equal algorithmic accuracy across patient groups can then inadvertently harm both groups, because a uniform accuracy requirement distorts the firm's investment incentives and the physician's equilibrium AI-use decisions.

</details>


### [116] [Your Probabilistic JEPA Is Secretly a Hidden Markov Model: A State-Space Interpretation of Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13621)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了时间索引的预测信息瓶颈JEPA (PIB-VJEPA)与隐马尔可夫模型(HMM)之间深层的结构对应关系，将随机上下文编码器、概率预测器和解码器分别映射为HMM中的滤波分布、状态转移和发射过程。作者提出4级递进对应层次并给出精确序列级HMM等价的充分条件，进而引入Markov-Chain JEPA (MCJEPA)作为具体实例，通过学习转移矩阵在有限时间齐次情形下保证多时间尺度Chapman-Kolmogorov一致性。实验验证了转移组合、滤波解释和预测马尔可夫化等主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然时间JEPA模型在自监督学习中取得了成功，但其潜在表征的动态行为缺乏严谨的理论解释。作者希望为时间JEPA提供有原则的状态空间解释，通过建立与经典HMM的结构对应关系，阐明JEPA各组件（编码器、预测器、解码器）在概率推断中的确切功能角色。

Method: 作者首先抽象出HMM的三重角色（滤波推断、马尔可夫转移、发射），指出PIB-VJEPA具有相同的计算结构。随后定义了4级递增的对应层次，并给出精确序列级HMM等价的充分条件。在此基础上提出MCJEPA，将潜在预测器替换为学习的转移矩阵，在有限时间齐次情形下通过矩阵幂确保多时间尺度Chapman-Kolmogorov一致性，并将构造推广到条件离散状态转移、连续状态马尔可夫核及连续时间动力学。预测信息瓶颈学习被解释为寻求紧致的预测状态：压缩促进极小性，残差可预测性测试充分性。

Result: 受控实验支持了转移组合性质、滤波解释、在已知合成过程中的预测马尔可夫化，以及对JEPA潜在预测与HMM风格序列学习之间区别的验证。这些结果共同为时间JEPA提供了有原则的状态空间解释。

Conclusion: 时间JEPA与HMM之间建立了清晰的结构对应关系，赋予了JEPA潜在表征以概率状态空间的理论基础。所提出的MCJEPA及其扩展（连续状态、连续时间）展示了该框架的普适性，而确定性JEPA则作为狄拉克核的退化特例被统一纳入。这为未来在JEPA框架下发展具有理论保证的时间建模方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: A hidden Markov model (HMM) combines three roles: inference of a hidden-state belief from observations, propagation through a Markov transition, and emission back to observation space. We show that full, time-indexed Predictive Information Bottleneck VJEPA (PIB-VJEPA) exposes the same computational structure: a stochastic context encoder plays the role of an amortized filtering distribution, a probabilistic predictor defines latent-state dynamics, and a decoder, inverse target encoder, or induced implicit conditional supplies the emission direction. We distinguish 4 progressively stronger levels of correspondence and give sufficient conditions for exact sequence-level HMM equivalence. To make the connection concrete, we introduce Markov-Chain JEPA (MCJEPA), which replaces the latent predictor by a learned transition matrix; in the finite time-homogeneous case, matrix powers guarantee exact multi-horizon Chapman--Kolmogorov consistency. Conditioned discrete-state transitions, continuous-state Markov kernels, and continuous-time dynamics extend this construction, while deterministic temporal JEPA appears as a degenerate Dirac-kernel special case. We further interpret predictive information-bottleneck learning as seeking a compact predictive state: compression promotes minimality, while residual predictability tests sufficiency. Controlled experiments support transition composition, the filtering interpretation, predictive Markovization in a known synthetic process, and the distinction between JEPA latent prediction and HMM-style sequence learning. Together, these results give temporal JEPA a principled state-space interpretation.

</details>


### [117] [ARC: Fair Relative Advantage Comparison in Open-Ended Real-World Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13622)
*Yongqi Tong,Tan Li Hui Faith,Choy Zhen Wen Marcus,Zhou Jin,Kewei Fu,Jiang-Ming Yang,Jianshe Li,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出开放世界交互中奖励比较公平性问题（reward fairness problem），并介绍ARC（基于条件化的优势正则化）训练方案结合策略条件化rollout分组、混合奖励和熵正则化来恢复公平的组内比较。同时提出INTER交互范式，将用户可见通信与潜在推理/工具使用解耦，并配套INTER-86K标注训练语料。实验表明ARC显著提升工具使用基准，INTER将首token延迟从4.91s降至1.27s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界交互中存在多种合理行为（如直接回答、询问澄清、更新进度、行动前确认），这使得基于群体的RL（group-based RL）的核心假设——组内rollout在行为上可比较——被打破。奖励模型对交互风格的偏好会扭曲相对优势估计，使优化偏向被奖励偏好的行为而非上下文合适的行为。

Method: 1) 形式化reward fairness problem；2) 提出ARC训练方案：通过策略条件化（strategy-conditioned）rollout分组恢复更公平的相对比较，结合混合奖励（hybrid rewards）和熵正则化；3) 提出INTER交互范式，解耦用户可见通信与潜在推理及工具使用；4) 构建INTER-86K策略标注训练语料，包含标注和蒸馏pipeline，用于监督学习和强化学习训练。

Result: ARC显著增强了核心τ/τ²工具使用基准的表现；INTER在交互场景中将time-to-first-token从4.91s降低至1.27s（相对think-style基线）。实验结果显示公平比较是提升开放交互学习的关键瓶颈，ARC实现和INTER-86K数据将开源。

Conclusion: 开放交互学习的核心瓶颈不仅在于agent如何被奖励，更在于其行为是否被公平比较。通过策略条件化分组使rollout可比，并结合解耦通信/推理的交互范式和高质量策略标注数据，能够在性能与响应延迟两方面同时取得显著改进。

Abstract: Open-ended real-world interaction admits multiple valid behaviors: an agent may answer directly, ask for clarification, provide progress updates, or confirm before acting. This flexibility breaks a core assumption behind group-based RL: rollouts compared within a group are no longer guaranteed to be behaviorally comparable. As a result, reward-model preferences over interaction style can distort relative advantages and steer optimization toward reward-preferred behaviors rather than context-appropriate ones. We formalize this as a \textit{reward fairness problem} and propose \textbf{ARC} (Advantage Regularization via Conditioning), a training recipe that restores fairer relative comparison through strategy-conditioned rollout grouping, together with hybrid rewards and entropy regularization. We study ARC in our proposed \inter, a novel paradigm for responsive, steerable, and execution-aware user-agent interaction that decouples user-visible communication from latent reasoning and tool use. \inter\ also provides the annotation and distillation pipeline for constructing \inter-86K, our strategy-annotated training corpus for supervised and RL training. Empirically, ARC substantially strengthens the core $τ/τ^2$ tool-use benchmarks, while \inter\ reduces time-to-first-token from 4.91s to 1.27s relative to a think-style baseline. Together, these results suggest that a central bottleneck in open-ended interactive learning is not only how agents are rewarded, but whether their behaviors are compared fairly in the first place. The ARC implementation and \inter-86K training data will be released.

</details>


### [118] [Reward Machines for Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13625)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Shangtong Zhang,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自动机的STL规范强化学习方法，通过构建时间交替自动机作为记忆机制并提供马尔可夫奖励，解决传统基于鲁棒性分数的奖励在长时域任务中状态空间爆炸的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STL规范在控制综合中具有重要应用，但现代自主与AI系统缺乏精确完整的系统模型，使得优化方法不适用而需依赖强化学习。然而，现有使用STL鲁棒性分数作为奖励的方法依赖执行历史，导致长时域、任意嵌套时态算子规范下的状态空间急剧膨胀，难以处理。

Method: 从给定STL规范构建时间交替自动机（timed alternating automaton），将自动机位置和时钟估值扩充到状态空间中，并从自动机接受条件推导出符合马尔可夫性质的奖励函数，从而为强化学习提供高效记忆机制。

Result: 实验表明，该方法学习到的策略在鲁棒性分数和满足率上均优于现有基于鲁棒性奖励的方法。

Conclusion: 基于自动机的记忆机制和马尔可夫奖励是处理长时域STL控制综合问题的有效方案，能显著优于传统鲁棒性奖励方法。

Abstract: Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for specifying real-time properties of real-valued observations, along with a quantitative robustness score for monitoring satisfaction. Control synthesis from STL specifications is of interest since manual controller design becomes infeasible as real-world systems grow in complexity. Moreover, many modern autonomous and AI-enabled systems lack accurate and complete system models, which makes optimization-based synthesis approaches unsuitable and motivates learning-based control. Prior work uses STL robustness scores as rewards in reinforcement learning (RL) to obtain control policies satisfying given specifications; however, robustness depends on execution history, leading to intractable state space expansion for general long-horizon specifications with arbitrarily nested temporal operators. This work introduces a novel automata-based approach that provides an efficient memory mechanism and associated Markovian rewards suitable for RL frameworks. Our approach constructs a timed alternating automaton from the given STL specifications, augments the state space with automaton locations and clock valuations, and derives rewards from the automaton acceptance condition. We empirically demonstrate that our approach learns policies that achieve higher robustness scores and satisfaction rates than those learned by existing approaches using robustness-based rewards.

</details>


### [119] [A Calibrated Test of Internal Action Maps: State Signals Without Global Affine Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13626)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过证据格（evidence lattice）系统检验“隐藏状态信号可解码或可因果使用”与“可复用动作映射（action map）”之间的可分离性。研究发现：在仿射 S_5 载波上，所有保留源的折叠均能通过单步、复合、逆、解码和交换门；但在后训练的 Qwen/Qwen3-4B 模型中，冻结的最终 token h28 仿射映射的保持实体误差为 .519（对比测试域内交叉拟合 .398），且七种随机实体划分与映射几何不支持纯实体特定解释。早期 h4/h16 层单步转换更佳但冲突态解码弱，因果效应仅出现在 h28/h36。尽管结果感知重拟合改善 h28 单步误差，但无任何重拟合通过复合门，说明状态可用性、因果使用、局部几何与可复用闭包在测试载波内是可分离的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是厘清一个理论问题：隐藏状态信号可被解码或在因果上使用，并不必然意味着存在可复用的动作映射。作者希望区分“状态可用性”“因果使用”“局部几何”与“可复用闭包”这四个概念，从而避免将前者的存在等同于后者的成立。

Method: 构建证据格（evidence lattice）组织测试，包含单步、复合、逆、解码、交换性等门控检验。首先在已知仿射 S_5 载波上验证几何分支，然后在后训练的 Qwen/Qwen3-4B 中对冻结的最终 token 层（h28）及更早层（h4/h16）进行仿射映射拟合，采用七种随机实体划分评估泛化；同时从同一冻结检查点生成三个匹配的干预数据集以检验因果效应，并进行结果感知重拟合（含加权）以及学习有限世界的比较实验。

Result: S_5 载波的所有保留源折叠通过全部五个门；结构化曲率和保持域共轭单调地提高误差，但仅 23/30 最强单元翻转闭包门，表明校准是受限的而非普适的。Qwen3-4B 中 h28 保持实体误差 .519 vs 测试域内 .398；七种随机划分与几何不支持纯实体特定解释。h4/h16 单步拟合更好但 h4 冲突态解码弱且词汇控制未解决。因果效应仅在 h28/h36 出现。结果感知重拟合将 h28 单步误差降至 .474（加权 .469），但无重拟合通过复合门。

Conclusion: 在测试的载波范围内，状态可用性、因果使用、局部几何与可复用闭包是相互分离的：即便某个层能解码状态或产生因果效应，也不代表其仿射动作映射能构成可复用的封闭结构。该结论受限于单一预训练模型、采样的最终 token 层、两个有限世界及所测仿射/诊断函数类。

Abstract: A hidden state signal can be decodable or causally usable without supporting a reusable action map. We test whether action maps fitted without a source reach its natural post-action activation and compose. We organize the tests as an evidence lattice and validate the geometric branch on a known affine S_5 carrier: all held-source folds pass one-step, composition, inverse, decoding, and commutativity gates. Structured curvature and held-domain conjugacy raise error monotonically, but only 23/30 strongest cells flip a closure gate, bounding rather than universalizing calibration. In post-trained Qwen/Qwen3-4B, frozen final-token h28 affine maps have mean held-entity error .519, versus .398 for within-test-domain cross-fit. Seven randomized entity splits and map geometry do not support a purely entity-specific account. Earlier h4/h16 layers fit one-step transitions better, but h4 conflict-state decoding is weak and lexical controls remain unresolved. Three matched intervention datasets regenerated from one frozen checkpoint show causal effects only at h28/h36. Outcome-aware refitting improves h28 one-step error to .474 (.469 with weighting), yet no refit passes composition. Learned finite worlds likewise preserve relative algebraic signals or shared charts without held-source affine closure. Within the tested carriers, state availability, causal use, local geometry, and reusable closure are separable. The result is limited to one pretrained model, sampled final-token layers, two finite worlds, and the tested affine or diagnostic function classes.

</details>


### [120] [Exploring ESC Winners with Nested Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13630)
*Anurag Sharma,Marcel Nöhre,Gerd Stumme*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConceptFlow是一个兼容scikit-learn的Python库，用于形式概念分析，能够从多值形式背景中构建和渲染嵌套线图。作者将其应用于1975至2025年欧洲歌唱大赛的获奖者数据，通过概念尺度的划分（外交尺度捕捉投票模式，内层尺度捕捉音乐特征），生成嵌套线图以揭示投票模式与音乐特征之间的跨尺度依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式概念分析中的多值形式背景需要概念缩放来处理，而现有工具缺乏对嵌套线图（子直积）的可视化支持。作者希望通过提供一个易用、兼容scikit-learn生态的库，使研究者能够直观地探索多尺度概念之间的蕴含关系。

Method: ConceptFlow库的实现流程：输入多值形式背景，将属性划分为概念尺度（外尺度捕捉投票支持的区域/文化/历史/政治维度，内尺度捕捉调性和节奏等音乐特征），执行概念缩放，计算因子格，识别子直积中的填充节点，最后生成交互式嵌套线图可视化。将其应用于欧歌赛1975-2025年获奖数据。

Result: 生成的嵌套线图揭示了外交尺度（投票模式）与内层尺度（音乐特征）之间的跨尺度蕴含关系，展示了获奖作品的投票支持模式与其共享的音乐属性之间存在的依赖关系。

Conclusion: ConceptFlow为形式概念分析提供了一个实用的、可交互的库工具，并通过欧歌赛案例证明了嵌套线图在揭示多维度概念依赖方面的有效性，为未来在更大规模数据集上的概念分析提供了工具基础。

Abstract: We present ConceptFlow, a scikit-learn-compatible Python library for Formal Concept Analysis that constructs and renders nested line diagrams from many-valued formal contexts. Given a many-valued context and a partition of its attributes into conceptual scales, ConceptFlow performs conceptual scaling, computes the factor lattices, identifies filled nodes of the corresponding subdirect product, and produces an interactive visualization.
  We apply ConceptFlow to the winners of the Eurovision Song Contest from 1975 to 2025, exploring relationships between voting patterns and musical characteristics. Voting support is captured by an outer scale spanning regional, cultural, historical, and political dimensions, while an inner scale captures musical characteristics via tempo and key. The resulting nested line diagram reveals implications across both scales, exposing dependencies between how winning entries were voted for and the musical properties they share.

</details>


### [121] [Ontology-Grounded Project Memory for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13662)
*James Adam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍MOOSEDev，一个为编码智能体提供基于本体（ontology）的结构化项目记忆系统。该通过知识图谱捕获架构决策、经验教训、约束与理由，并通过Model Context Protocol（MCP）接口暴露给智能体。系统采用专有的神经符号引擎MOOSE，以符号层为主要推理基质。在835条类型化记录的公共语料上，MOOSEDev在取代关系、集合完备性和否定类问题的期望答案召回上接近满分（0.98-1.00），而基线向量记忆的top-k检索仅覆盖6%-27%；但在相关性召回与token成本上两者基本相当。论文还描述了时间戳提交历史的数据引导、预注册的实时试验及经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体已成为许多软件项目中新代码生成的主要方式，变更速度之快使得追踪变更背后的原因变得极具挑战性。现有系统缺乏结构化、本体化的项目记忆，无法让智能体理解架构决策、约束与理由等深层语义信息。

Method: MOOSEDev构建一个承载架构决策、教训、约束与理由的知识图谱作为项目记忆，通过Model Context Protocol（MCP）接口向编码智能体暴露。记录带有生命周期状态、来源及取代关系链接。查询由专有神经符号引擎MOOSE完成，该引擎将符号层作为主要推理基质。评估使用公共语料库（835条类型化记录），与生产级向量记忆工具进行对比。

Result: 在取代关系、集合完备性和否定类问题上，MOOSEDev的期望答案召回接近完全（0.98-1.00），而基线向量检索的top-k结果仅覆盖6%-27%。在相关性召回与token成本方面两者基本相当。

Conclusion: MOOSEDev表明符号化、本体驱动的项目记忆在特定类型的推理问题上显著优于纯向量记忆方案，同时不牺牲相关性召回和token效率。论文还提供了时间戳提交历史的引导方法、预注册实时试验的设计及实际部署中的经验教训，为编码智能体的项目记忆系统提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Coding agents have become the primary means of generating new code in many software projects, and the resulting velocity of changes makes keeping track of the reasons behind those changes challenging. This paper introduces MOOSEDev, a system designed to give coding agents structured, ontology-grounded project memory. The system captures architectural decisions, lessons, constraints, and rationales in a knowledge graph exposed to agents via a Model Context Protocol (MCP) interface. Records carry lifecycle status, provenance, and supersession links, queryable via MOOSE, a proprietary neurosymbolic engine that treats the symbolic layer as the primary reasoning substrate. We compared MOOSEDev against a production vector-memory tool on a neutral public corpus of 835 typed records. MOOSEDev returned the expected answer set essentially in full (0.98-1.00) on supersession, set-completeness, and negation questions, whereas the baseline's top-k retrieval surfaced between 6% and 27%. Conversely, relevance recall and token cost were largely equivalent between the two systems. We also describe a temporal commit-history bootstrap of our own codebase, a pre-registered live trial, and lessons learned.

</details>


### [122] [Second Thought: Reasoning in Parallel as LLM Agents Act and Observe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13667)
*Zhensu Sun,Chengran Yang,Yunbo Lyu,Jieke Shi,David Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Second Thought，一种无需训练（training-free）的推理框架。它在ReAct范式中Thought阶段结束时并行fork出四个辅助分支，与主循环并发解码，并在环境观测返回时合并生成的思考。该方法将额外推理从主线程的顺序解码路径中移出，在三个agentic基准和三个推理LLM上，所有九个（模型,基准）组合均降低了平均轮次，六个组合中主线程解码最多减少43%（平均约20%），Pass@1在七个组合中无显著变化，两个显著差异为+12.4和+10.2分。与计算匹配的控制组相比，在所有适用的四个设置中，Pass@1严格更高且顺序解码少1.3到3.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct范式的LLM agent在思考（Thought）与行动/观察（Action/Observation）之间交替，但深入推理仅限于Thought阶段：当agent序列化动作并等待环境响应时，其推理处于冻结状态。作者识别出Action和Observation之间存在一个"推理空闲窗口"（reasoning idle window），并问是否可以利用这段空闲时间进行并行推理以服务未来的轮次。核心动机是充分利用推理过程中的空闲计算资源来提升agent效率与性能。

Method: 提出Second Thought，一个无需训练的推理框架。具体而言，在每次Thought阶段结束的瞬间fork出四个辅助推理分支，这些分支与主循环并发解码（decode），生成额外的思考内容；当环境观测（observation）返回时，将辅助分支生成的思考合并回主循环。通过这种方式，额外的推理被移动到主线程的顺序解码路径之外，相当于在主线程等待环境响应的空闲窗口内并行执行推理。

Result: 在三个agentic基准和三个推理LLM上评估：Second Thought在所有九个（模型,基准）组合中均降低了平均turn数；在六个组合中减少了主线程解码量，最高减少43%，这些设置中平均约减少20%；在第七个组合中主线程解码基本不变。Pass@1在九个组合中的七个无显著变化，两个显著差异分别为+12.4和+10.2分。与计算匹配的控制组（将同等计算预算强制用于主线程推理）对比，在所有适用的四个设置中，Second Thought的Pass@1严格更高，且顺序解码量少1.3到3.2倍。

Conclusion: Second Thought验证了推理空闲窗口可以被有效利用：通过将辅助推理并行化到主线程之外，在不显著牺牲任务成功率（甚至在某些情况下提升Pass@1）的前提下，大幅减少主线程的顺序解码时间和平均轮次，从而提升LLM agent的整体推理效率和响应速度。

Abstract: LLM agents in the ReAct paradigm alternate between reasoning, acting, and observing, but deliberate reasoning is confined to the Thought phase: while the agent serializes an action and waits for the environment, its reasoning is frozen. We identify this recurring interval for Action and Observation as a reasoning idle window and ask whether it can host additional reasoning in parallel that serves future turns. Therefore, we propose Second Thought, a training-free inference framework that forks four auxiliary branches the instant each Thought phase concludes, decodes them concurrently with the main loop, and merges the generated thoughts back when the environment observation arrives. In this way, Second Thought relocates the added reasoning off the main thread's sequential decoding path. Across three agentic benchmarks and three reasoning LLMs, Second Thought lowers the average turn count in all nine (model,benchmark) pairs and reduces main thread decoding in six of them by up to 43% (roughly 20% on average among those settings), while leaving it essentially unchanged in a seventh; Pass@1 shows no significant change in seven of nine pairs and the two significant differences are +12.4 and +10.2 points. Against a compute-matched control that forces an equivalent budget onto the main thread's own reasoning, it attains strictly higher Pass@1 with 1.3 to 3.2 less sequential decoding in all four settings where the control applies.

</details>


### [123] [Learning to Assemble Novel Structures with Unfamiliar Parts under Semantic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13684)
*Jonghyuk Park,Alex Lascarides,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号架构，使智能体能够通过具身对话和任务演示学习组装新结构，应对部署后出现的未见过的语义约束。在模拟玩具卡车组装域中，实验表明通过自然语言传达语义约束比仅依赖演示或仅命名部件更具数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中，智能体在部署后可能遇到训练时未见过的新语义约束（如哪些部件类型和特征构成有效结构），且初始对这些概念一无所知。智能体需要通过用户交互在线获取并利用这些知识来完成组装任务，这要求探索一种高效的在线适应机制。

Method: 提出一种神经符号（neurosymbolic）架构，结合符号推理与神经网络学习，融合两种证据来源：自然语言中的符号证据和密集视觉观测。智能体通过对话和任务演示在组装过程中与用户交互，逐步获取结构约束知识并进行在线适应。

Result: 实验表明，通过自然语言直接传达语义约束（如"dump trucks have a dumper"）比仅依赖任务演示或仅用自然语言命名部件能实现更高效的数据在线适应。

Conclusion: 在组装任务中，利用自然语言显式传达语义约束可以显著提升智能体的在线学习效率和适应能力，神经符号架构为处理部署后新语义约束的具身任务提供了有效范式。

Abstract: This paper describes a neurosymbolic architecture for learning to assemble novel structures using evidence from embodied conversations and task demonstrations. We focus on scenarios where an agent encounters, after deployment, semantic constraints on structures--in other words, constraints as to which part types and features make valid structures--that were not available during training, and where it is initially unaware of the relevant structure and component part concepts. The agent must acquire and exploit such knowledge through user interactions while attempting assembly. We study this setting in a simulated toy truck assembly domain, learning from symbolic evidence encoded in natural language and from dense visual observations. Our experiments show that communicating semantic constraints through natural language (e.g., "dump trucks have a dumper") yields more data-efficient online adaptation than relying only on task demonstrations and/or only naming the parts through natural language.

</details>


### [124] [Coverage Aware Active Evaluation for Failure Discovery with Paired Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13719)
*Anjali Parashar,Rachel Luo,Apoorva Sharma,Sushant Veer,Edward Schmerling,Carson Sobolewski,Mingxin Yu,Chuchu Fan,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应故障发现方法，利用代理系统（仿真器、低保真系统或相关策略）的大量评估结果，结合有限的目标系统测试结果，通过控制变量启发式的残差建模来预测目标系统风险，并采用支持感知的互信息目标来发现既可能发生又多样化的故障，在自动驾驶、操作和四足速度跟踪任务中比基线方法多发现高达2倍的故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的自主系统故障具有稀有且异构的特点，在有限的测试预算下难以发现。虽然仿真器等代理系统可以大量采样以发现故障，但代理系统与真实系统之间存在sim-to-real及系统间差距，导致代理故障往往无法迁移到真实世界。因此需要有效利用代理系统信息来准确预测目标系统的严重故障。

Method: 提出自适应故障发现方法：(1) 结合代理评估与有限目标系统结果，采用控制变量（control-variate）启发的残差建模学习目标风险的局部预测器，修正代理故障信号；(2) 将预测器与支持感知的互信息目标相结合，该目标偏好现实且充分支持的场景区域，同时扩展故障模式的覆盖范围，从而发现既可能发生又多样化的故障。

Result: 在自动驾驶、操纵和四足速度跟踪三个任务上，该方法比随机采样和主动学习基线多发现高达2倍的故障，包括竞争方法遗漏的严重且多样化的故障。

Conclusion: 通过残差建模校正代理信号并结合支持感知的多样性目标，可以有效利用代理系统信息在有限预算下发现目标系统的严重且多样化的故障，优于随机采样和主动学习基线。

Abstract: Autonomous systems can fail in rare and heterogeneous ways, making real-world failure discovery difficult under limited testing budgets. Although cheaper proxies such as simulators, lower-fidelity systems, or related policies can be sampled extensively to find failures, proxy failures often do not transfer to the real world due to sim-to-real and system-to-system gaps. The key challenge is therefore to effectively leverage proxy system information for accurate prediction of severe target system failures. We propose an adaptive failure discovery method that combines proxy evaluations with limited target system results to guide scenario selection for target system testing. Our method learns a local predictor of target risk by correcting proxy failure signals using control-variate-inspired residual modeling. To find failures that are both likely and diverse, we combine this predictor with a support-aware mutual-information objective that favors realistic, well-supported regions while expanding coverage across failure modes. Across autonomous driving, manipulation, and quadruped velocity-tracking tasks, our method discovers up to 2$\times$ as many failures as random sampling and active-learning baselines, including severe and diverse failures missed by competing methods.

</details>


### [125] [Explanation Multiplicity: Circuit-Level Interpretability Evidence Does Not Survive Defensible Analytic Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13754)
*Ajay Pravin Mahale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究使用预注册的15,840种规范配置，在GPT-2 small上对间接对象识别任务进行电路发现，发现欧盟AI法案要求的高风险系统技术文档（Annex IV声明）在73.2%的规范对中会发生翻转，即使标准化评估指标后翻转率仍达59.4%，移除电路大小后仍为27.1%。底层电路在结构上近乎不相交（Jaccard重叠中位数4%）且功能上不相关（Cohen's kappa 0.015），表明这种不稳定性并非同一机制的不同表述。作者提出"可归档性标准"作为独立协议，并发现七个文档化发现目标之一在其库的规范任务上根本无法执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案要求高风险系统提供技术文档以说明决策机制，机械可解释性（尤其是电路发现）被视为此类证据的主要来源。但一个关键问题尚未检验：当两位称职的分析师使用相同系统、相同工具但选择不同的合理设置时，这些证据是否仍然成立？研究旨在检验电路发现证据在真实使用条件下（分析师可自由选择合理解析参数）的稳健性。

Method: 研究者预注册了一个跨7个分析轴的交叉网格，每个轴的所有水平均取自已发表实现。将发现的每个电路通过确定性声明映射转换为结构化的Annex IV声明。对GPT-2 small和间接对象识别任务运行了15,840个预注册规范，其中7,561个产生声明。计算声明在不同规范对之间的翻转率，并逐一检验标准化评估指标、移除电路大小等干预措施的影响。同时使用Jaccard重叠和Cohen's kappa评估底层电路的结构和功能一致性。

Result: 在7,561个产生声明的规范中，73.2%的规范对（95%CI 0.725-0.738）产生翻转的声明，众数声明仅占41.1%的空间。标准化最具影响力的选择（评估指标）后翻转率为59.4%；从声明中移除电路大小并固定后为27.1%，仍高于预注册阈值。底层电路的中位Jaccard重叠仅为4%，Cohen's kappa为0.015。此外，七个文档化发现目标之一在库自身的规范任务上完全无法执行。

Conclusion: 电路发现产生的证据在欧盟AI法案的归档标准下无法通过可归档性标准——在所有合理可接受的容差水平下均失败。证据的不稳定性并非同一机制的不同表述，而是结构上不同的电路。这意味着机械可解释性证据在目前状态下不足以支撑高风险AI系统的合规文档。研究仅覆盖一个模型和一个任务，结论在更大规模上是否成立尚未验证，这构成关键的后续工作方向。

Abstract: The EU AI Act requires providers of high-risk systems to file technical documentation describing how the system reaches its decisions. Mechanistic interpretability is the obvious source of such evidence, and circuit discovery is its most developed instrument. We ask whether that evidence survives the condition under which it would be relied upon: two competent analysts, the same system, the same tool, different defensible settings.
  We pre-registered a crossed grid of seven analytic axes, every level taken from a published implementation, and mapped each discovered circuit through a deterministic claim map to a structured Annex IV statement. Across 15,840 pre-registered specifications on GPT-2 small and the indirect object identification task, of which 7,561 produced a claim, the derived statement flips across 73.2% of specification pairs (95% CI 0.725 to 0.738) and the modal claim commands 41.1% of the space. The evidence fails a filability criterion at every tolerance a conformity assessment body would plausibly accept.
  Standardising the single most influential choice, the evaluation metric, leaves the flip rate at 59.4%. Removing circuit size from the claim entirely and holding it fixed leaves 27.1% (95% CI 0.255 to 0.286), still above the pre-registered threshold. The circuits underlying these claims are structurally near-disjoint, median pairwise Jaccard overlap 4%, and functionally uncorrelated at Cohen's kappa 0.015, so the instability is not one mechanism described in different words.
  We give the filability criterion as a standalone protocol, and we report that one of the seven documented discovery objectives does not execute at all on the library's own canonical task. The study covers one model and one task, and whether the conclusion holds at scale is untested.

</details>


### [126] [Simulation-Aware In-Context Policy Improvement for LLM-Aided Analog Layout Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13767)
*Bingyang Liu,Ziming Wei,Xiaohan Gao,David Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模拟感知的LLM多智能体框架，通过"行动-观察-反思"循环对模拟IC版图生成器的优化参数进行上下文内策略改进（ICPI），仅需几十次版图后仿真即可超越生成器内置启发式方法及贝叶斯优化方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟IC版图设计仍高度依赖人工迭代调参，端到端版图生成器虽能加速初始布局布线，但需专家手动微调优化参数并反复进行后仿真。现有的贝叶斯优化方法在版图层面需要数百到上千次评估，每次涉及昂贵的寄生提取和后仿真，实用性差；而LLM虽有提升样本效率的潜力，但缺乏几何版图上下文和设计特定启发式信息，难以操控版图优化过程。

Method: 提出一种模拟感知的LLM多智能体框架，采用上下文内策略改进（ICPI）方法，通过"行动-观察-反思"（act-observe-reflect）循环，在紧凑的结构化版图表示上迭代更新模拟版图生成器暴露的版图优化参数。

Result: 在真实模拟电路上的实验表明，该框架仅需数十次后版图仿真，即可在后版图性能上超越生成器内置启发式方法和基于贝叶斯优化的调参方法。

Conclusion: 结合LLM的多智能体上下文内策略改进能够显著提升模拟版图优化调参的样本效率，以极少的仿真代价获得优于传统内置启发式和BO方法的性能，为模拟IC版图自动化设计提供了新的高效途径。

Abstract: Analog IC layout design remains a labor-intensive iterative process dominated by simulation-driven refinement. Although end-to-end layout generators accelerate initial placement and routing, they still require experts to manually tune layout optimization parameters with repeated post-layout simulations for stringent design specifications. While Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely adopted for parameter tuning in analog IC design, at the layout level it typically requires hundreds to thousands of evaluations, each involving costly parasitic extraction and post-layout simulation, which makes it impractical. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated potential in improving the sample efficiency of such simulation-driven tuning. However, their restricted access to geometric layout context and design-specific heuristics limits their ability to manipulate the layout optimization process. In this paper, we propose a simulation-aware LLM multi-agent framework that performs in-context policy improvement (ICPI) by iteratively updating layout optimization parameters exposed by an analog layout generator through an act-observe-reflect loop on compact structured layout representations. Experiments on real-world analog circuits show that, with only tens of post-layout simulations, our approach improves post-layout performance over the generator's built-in heuristics and BO-based tuning method.

</details>


### [127] [FLARE MCMC: Fidelity-based Layer-Adaptive REcursive proposals for MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13774)
*Harini Venkatesan,Christian Shelton,Ming-Feng Ho,Simeon Bird,Mengxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLARE MCMC是一种多保真度分层马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法，利用真实似然计算的低保真度近似来改善混合性能并降低整体计算成本，在多个科学领域（如水文学和宇宙学）中实现了相同计算时间下更大的有效样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MCMC方法仅需评估似然即可进行推断，但存在混合速率慢的问题，需要生成大量样本才能获得良好估计，导致计算成本高昂。科学和工程应用中常见可调节分辨率或精度的模拟模型，这类低保真度似然尚未被充分利用来加速MCMC推断。

Method: FLARE MCMC采用递归的分层链结构，利用真实似然计算的低保真度近似来改善混合速率。该技术使用简单的层参数调整，不要求似然具有任何特定形式或内部数学结构，具有广泛的适用性。

Result: 实验结果表明，FLARE MCMC在多个科学领域的应用中（包括水文学和宇宙学），在相同计算时间内实现了更大的有效样本量（ESS），证明了其在计算效率上的显著优势。

Conclusion: FLARE MCMC通过多保真度分层策略有效解决了传统MCMC混合慢、计算成本高的问题，且由于其不依赖似然的具体数学结构，具有通用性和广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) requires only the ability to evaluate the likelihood, making it a common technique for inference in complex models. However, it can have a slow mixing rate, requiring the generation of many samples to obtain good estimates and an overall high computational cost. FLARE MCMC is a multi-fidelity layered MCMC method that exploits lower-fidelity approximations of the true likelihood calculation to improve mixing and leads to overall faster performance. Such lower-fidelity likelihoods are commonly available in scientific and engineering applications where the model involves a simulation whose resolution or accuracy can be tuned. Our technique uses recursive, layered chains with simple layer tuning; it does not require the likelihood to take any form or have any particular internal mathematical structure. We demonstrate experimentally that FLARE MCMC achieves larger effective sample sizes for the same computational time across different scientific domains including hydrology and cosmology.

</details>


### [128] [From Passive Delegates to Strategic Negotiators: Reinforcing Social Reasoning in Small Language Models with SocialRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13787)
*Wenyue Hua,Zachary Huang,Tyler Payne,Safoora Yousefi,Saleema Amershi,Asli Celikyilmaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SocialRL，一种直接训练社交推理的通用方法，应用于4B模型覆盖六个社交谈判领域。实验发现：域内训练使4B模型达到前沿水平（匹配或超过GPT-5系列）；跨域迁移遵循博弈结构；结合级联RL和多教师在线策略蒸馏可将各域专家整合为统一4B模型，达到0.627平均效用，超越GPT-4.1等基线；显式心智理论脚手架仅在训练时有益，且只有下一动作预测技能能预测谈判结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体日益代表用户执行社交谈判任务（如日程安排、比价、议价），但其"讨喜"的助理性特质使其成为糟糕的代理人——会主动泄露委托人的私有信息并轻易让步。现有前沿模型缺乏为代理场景设计的社交推理能力，需要一种直接训练社交推理的方法。

Method: 提出SocialRL训练配方：(1) 在六个领域（Deal-or-No-Deal、CaSiNo、Craigslist、Job Interview、Calendar、Marketplace）进行域内直接训练；(2) 利用跨域迁移结构，采用级联RL（cascade RL）和多教师在线策略蒸馏（OPD）两种策略，将各域专家模型整合为单一统一模型；(3) 引入显式心智理论（ToM）脚手架，蒸馏ToM轨迹而非仅动作。

Result: (1) 域内训练4B模型在held-out场景匹配或超越GPT-5系列，在谈判博弈中弥合73-122%的基线到前沿差距，78%买方开价锚定在目标以下（未训练仅3%）；(2) 跨域迁移遵循博弈结构，结构配对的游戏互相促进；统一4B达到0.627平均效用，超越GPT-4.1(0.625)、GPT-5.1(0.619)和GPT-5.2(0.613)；(3) 蒸馏ToM轨迹提升所有环境效用并改善泛化。

Conclusion: 社交推理可以直接通过训练获得，而非仅依赖模型规模；跨域迁移的收益由博弈结构决定；显式心智理论脚手架在训练阶段至关重要，但推理时不需要显式执行。这为构建既可靠又高效的用户代理智能体提供了通用配方。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act on their users' behalf, handling tasks such as scheduling meetings, comparing offers, and haggling over prices. These principal-driven tasks routinely place the agent across from a counterpart (another user's agent, a seller, a recruiter) whose goals may conflict with its principal's. Yet the dispositions that make an assistant pleasant can make it a poor delegate: a friendly, helpful frontier model may disclose its principal's private information unprompted and concede at the first sign of resistance. We present SocialRL, a general recipe that trains social reasoning directly, and apply it to a 4B model across six domains: Deal-or-No-Deal, CaSiNo, Craigslist, Job Interview, Calendar, and Marketplace. Every domain is trained in-domain under the same recipe, and every policy is evaluated on all six. We find that (1) in-domain training reaches the frontier: on held-out scenarios the 4B matches or exceeds the GPT-5 family per domain, closing 73-122% of the baseline-to-frontier gap on the negotiation games, with 78% of buyer openings anchoring below target versus 3% untrained; (2) cross-domain transfer follows game structure: structurally paired games lift each other, a broad multi-issue donor lifts nearly all domains, and structurally isolated games transfer nothing; (3) guided by this transfer structure, two strategies, cascade RL and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (OPD), consolidate the per-domain specialists into a single unified 4B that reaches 0.627 average utility across all six environments, matching or exceeding GPT-4.1 (0.625), GPT-5.1 (0.619), and GPT-5.2 (0.613); (4) an explicit theory-of-mind scaffold helps only through training: distilling the ToM trace, rather than actions alone, lifts utility on every environment and generalizes better across them, and of the two ToM skills, only next-action prediction predicts negotiation outcomes.

</details>


### [129] [SDO: Subspace Deconflicting Operator for Multi-Adapter Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13820)
*Zhongsheng Wang,Zhedong Lin,Qian Liu,Xinyu Zhang,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SDO（子空间去冲突算子），用于解决多适配器组合在共享扩散骨干网络中的干扰问题，通过重构低秩更新、提取子空间签名、量化输出子空间重叠并施加置换等变变换来抑制有害共享方向，同时保留身份特定特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立训练的适配器组合部署到共享扩散骨干网络时，常常出现身份混合、跨角色属性泄漏和场景组合不稳定等问题。作者从参数空间视角出发，假设这些干扰部分源于共享层中重叠的主子空间之间的冲突。

Method: SDO包括四个步骤：1) 从所选适配器重构逐层低秩更新；2) 提取紧凑子空间签名；3) 通过输出子空间重叠度量成对冲突；4) 应用置换等变变换抑制有害共享方向同时保留身份特定特征。最终将得到的表示映射回标准适配器更新，可直接集成到现有扩散推理流程中。

Result: 实验表明SDO持续提升了身份保真度和组合稳定性，尤其是在联合组合适配器数量增加时增益更为明显。

Conclusion: SDO提供了一种模块化的多适配器组合去冲突方案，在参数空间层面有效缓解了多角色生成中的干扰问题，并且可以直接嵌入现有扩散推理流水线。

Abstract: Composing independently trained adapters within a shared diffusion backbone provides a modular approach to multi-character generation, but naive joint deployment often causes identity mixing, cross-character attribute leakage, and unstable scene composition. We study this interference from a parameter-space perspective and hypothesize that it arises partly from conflicts between overlapping dominant subspaces in shared layers. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{SDO}, a \textbf{S}ubspace \textbf{D}econflicting \textbf{O}perator for multi-adapter composition. SDO reconstructs layer-wise low-rank updates from the selected adapters, extracts compact subspace signatures, measures pairwise conflict through output-subspace overlap, and applies a permutation-equivariant transformation that suppresses harmful shared directions while retaining identity-specific characteristics. The resulting representations are mapped back to standard adapter updates and can be directly incorporated into existing diffusion inference pipelines. Experiments demonstrate that SDO consistently improves identity fidelity and compositional stability, with particularly clear gains as the number of jointly composed adapters increases.

</details>


### [130] [Joint Optimization of Memory and Computing Frequency for Energy-Efficient DNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13863)
*Yunchu Han,Zhaojun Nan,Sheng Zhou,Zhisheng Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合优化计算频率、内存频率和通信资源的框架，以实现移动设备上能耗高效的深度神经网络（DNN）推理。通过凸优化推导出本地推理的近似最优闭式解，并为边缘推理获得传输功率的最优闭式解，同时提出低复杂度启发式算法求解整体问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备上的DNN推理因有限的计算和内存资源而面临高延迟和高能耗问题。现有研究主要关注通过DVFS调节计算频率来降低能耗，但忽视了内存频率对推理性能的重要影响，缺乏对两者联合优化的研究。

Method: 提出基于真实推理时间模型（同时考虑内存频率和计算频率）的能量最小化优化问题，在截止时间约束下联合优化频率和通信资源。对本地推理场景采用凸优化推导近最优闭式解；对边缘推理场景在给定带宽下推导传输功率的最优闭式解；并设计低复杂度多项式时间启发式算法求解整体问题。

Result: 基于实测数据的仿真结果表明，所提出的本地推理近最优解在严格截止时间约束下可达到最优性能，与最优解的差距不超过2.5%；总体算法相比其他方法可将设备能耗降低高达10.4%。

Conclusion: 联合考虑内存频率与计算频率能够显著提升移动端DNN推理的能效，所提算法在接近最优性能的同时具备低计算复杂度，为实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Deep neural network (DNN) inference on mobile devices often incurs high latency and energy consumption due to limited computing and memory resources. To enable energy-efficient DNN inference, most existing studies focus on dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS) for adjusting the computing frequency, while the impact of memory frequency on the inference performance has been greatly overlooked. In this paper, we consider the impact of memory frequency and computing frequency on DNN inference time, and jointly optimize these two frequencies together with communication resources for energy-efficient DNN inference. Based on a realistic inference time model, we formulate an optimization problem to minimize the energy consumption of all mobile devices under the deadline constraint. For local inference, we derive a near-optimal closed-form solution via convex optimization, while an optimal closed-form solution for transmission power is obtained for edge inference with the given bandwidth. Furthermore, we propose a low-complexity heuristic algorithm to effectively solve the overall problem with polynomial time complexity. Simulation results based on measured data show that the proposed near-optimal solution for local inference can achieve optimal performance under strict deadline constraints, with a performance gap of up to 2.5% compared with the optimal solution. Meanwhile, our proposed algorithm significantly reduces the energy consumption of devices by up to 10.4% compared to other methods.

</details>


### [131] [MemoryLake on MemoryArena: A Matched Study of Agent Memory Backends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13883)
*Chaoqun Zhan,Qiang Zhou,Guannan Li,Zhenqiang Huang,Qianjin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍MemoryArena基准与MemoryLake多轨记忆后端，对比Mem0、文本嵌入向量RAG和长上下文控制三种系统。MemoryLake在数学、物理和渐进式检索上表现最优，最优平均成功率达20.5%，但样本量有限、置信区间重叠，仅支持工作负载依赖的观点而非基准级先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent记忆基准多测试事后回忆（post-hoc recall），缺乏对跨会话、相互依赖任务完成能力的评估。本文旨在通过MemoryArena基准，系统评估不同记忆后端在多会话任务中支持程度，弥补现有基准的不足。

Method: 构建MemoryArena基准，覆盖五个领域（数学、物理、渐进式检索、旅行规划、网络购物）。在相同Agent框架下，仅改变记忆后端作为受控变量，对比MemoryLake（结构化多轨记忆）、Mem0、text-embedding-3-small向量RAG和长上下文控制。采用系统级匹配比较（非表示消融或成本匹配实验），使用成功率（SR）及软过程分数作为评估指标。

Result: MemoryLake在数学（9/40）、物理（12/20）和渐进式检索（4/20）上成功率最高。旅行规划所有系统均为零成功率，网络购物仅有长上下文取得1/150的包级成功。MemoryLake在后两项的过程分数上排名第三。套件级加权平均SR为20.5% vs 最佳对照13.6%。仅MemoryLake的221个查询运行得到26.7%成功率（非基线对比）。所有结果为点估计，置信区间重叠。

Conclusion: 结果支持工作负载依赖的记忆后端观点，MemoryLake在共享测试集上于四个系统中表现领先，但不构成基准级最先进水平，也不证明表征结构的因果优势。研究强调记忆集成是系统级因素，未来需要更大样本量以确认统计显著性。

Abstract: Most agent-memory benchmarks test post-hoc recall, whereas MemoryArena evaluates whether memory supports interdependent, multi-session task completion. We compare MemoryLake, a structured multi-track memory backend, with Mem0, text-embedding-3-small vector RAG, and a long-context control across all five MemoryArena domains. The systems share the same agent framework, requested gpt-5-mini model alias, task samples, and scoring code; the memory integration is the intentionally changed component. Because each backend bundles write, retrieval, consolidation, budgeting, and prompt-assembly choices, the study is a matched system-level comparison, not a representation-only ablation or a cost-matched experiment. On the shared evaluation sets, MemoryLake has the highest observed success rate (SR) in mathematics (9/40), physics (12/20), and progressive retrieval (4/20). Every system has zero SR in travel planning, and web shopping yields a single bundle-level success (long context, 1/150); MemoryLake ranks third on both the travel soft process score and shopping step match. Following MemoryArena's suite-level convention, a post-hoc equal-weight average over the five SRs is 20.5% for MemoryLake versus 13.6% for the best comparator. These are point estimates: sample sizes are modest, confidence intervals overlap, and we do not report paired significance tests. A separate MemoryLake-only run over all 221 progressive queries yields a failure-counted SR of 26.7% (59/221) and is not a baseline comparison. The results support a workload-dependent view of memory backends and an observed lead among the four evaluated systems on the shared sets; they do not establish benchmark-wide state of the art or a causal advantage of representation structure.

</details>


### [132] [When Personal Memory Has No Single Answer: Evaluating LLM Agents under Irreducible Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13921)
*Lu Yang,Shusheng Xu,Zhuoran Li,Tongkai Yang,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TANGLE基准，用于评估LLM智能体在面临真正不可解决的记忆冲突时的表现。该基准包含541个实例、40个角色，涵盖三种冲突类型（上下文分区冲突、行为振荡冲突、来源矛盾冲突），并在五个维度上评估模型能力。实验发现模型在识别冲突上表现较好，但在行动校准和寻求澄清方面不足；端到端管道中记忆提取无法保留冲突关系。作者提出Conflict-Aware Action Policy（CAAP）策略来适配不同冲突类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体会跨会话维护个人记忆，但记忆之间可能产生冲突。现有的基准在处理冲突证据时只恢复一个答案，忽视了智能体是否能够识别问题的不确定性、保留备选方案、寻求缺失信息并选择适当行动的能力。作者旨在填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建TANGLE基准，包含541个实例、40个角色，三种冲突类型（CPC上下文分区冲突、BOC行为振荡冲突、SCC来源矛盾冲突）。设置两条评估轨道：oracle轨道使用精心策划的记忆，pipeline轨道从多会话对话中提取记忆。从五个维度评估：冲突感知、因果推理、置信度校准、澄清寻求和记忆保真度。并提出CAAP（Conflict-Aware Action Policy）作为自适应行动策略。

Result: 实验发现：在oracle设定下模型识别冲突比校准行动或寻求针对性澄清更可靠；端到端pipeline中记忆提取无法保留下游推理所需的冲突相关关系；固定规则在行动需反映冲突时不足。

Conclusion: TANGLE将冲突处理重新定义为：识别不确定性（underdetermination）、保留冲突证据、以及在不强制给出确定性答案的前提下采取行动。这些发现为设计自适应冲突行动策略（CAAP）提供了动机和基础。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly maintain personal memory across sessions, but it can conflict. Preferences depend on context, behavior evolves, and sources can conflict. When a query lacks context, time, or source authority to interpret conflict, treating one memory as definitive converts unresolved conflict into an unjustified, overconfident action. Existing benchmarks recover one answer from conflicting evidence, overlooking whether agents recognize underdetermination, preserve alternatives, seek missing information, and choose appropriate actions. We introduce \underline{T}esting \underline{A}gents' \underline{N}avigation of \underline{G}enuine, \underline{L}atent, and \underline{E}ntangled Memory Conflicts (\textsc{TANGLE}), a benchmark for genuinely unresolvable memory conflicts. It comprises 541 instances across 40 personas and three types: Context-Partitioned Conflict (CPC), Behavior-Oscillation Conflict (BOC), and Source-Contradiction Conflict (SCC). We evaluate two tracks---an oracle track with curated memory and a pipeline track that extracts memory from multi-session dialogues---on five dimensions: conflict perception, causal reasoning, confidence calibration, clarification seeking, and memory faithfulness. Experiments reveal pipeline challenges. With curated memory, models recognize conflicts more reliably than they calibrate actions or seek targeted clarification. With end-to-end pipeline memory, extraction fails to preserve conflict-bearing relations needed for downstream reasoning. Policy comparisons show fixed rules are insufficient when actions must reflect conflict. These findings motivate Conflict-Aware Action Policy (CAAP), which adapts actions to each conflict using available evidence. \textsc{TANGLE} frames conflict handling as recognizing underdetermination, retaining conflicting evidence, and acting without forcing a definitive answer.

</details>


### [133] [Never the Number: Structural Abstention for AI Systems Whose Answers Are Consumed as Fact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13926)
*Zhelun,Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"信任内核+生成外壳"(trusted kernel with a generative shell)的架构模式，解决LLM文本到SQL系统在部署中因幻觉导致"流畅但错误"回答的可信度问题。核心原则是：可伪造组件只能影响系统回答"哪个问题"，绝不能影响"返回什么值"。通过结构化弃答(structural abstention)，系统对无法表达的请求明确拒绝而非近似回答，无需置信度估计。作者给出了实现无关的规格说明、五步决策流程，并在三个领域验证，报告了两年生产案例研究，以及在企业与可靠性基准上的对比结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM文本到SQL系统虽然让数据库自然语言接口重新可行，但在部署中存在严重问题：模型幻觉出的列名或错误聚合会生成流畅却错误的答案，用户在使用时无法区分正确与错误回答。在无法检查生成查询的企业AI部署和运营仪表盘场景中，以及使用者是工具型代理而非人类时，仅凭准确性不够——没有机制标记哪些答案值得怀疑。这首先是可靠性问题，其次才是准确性问题。

Method: 提出"信任内核+生成外壳"架构模式，基于一个不变量：可以捏造内容的组件只能影响系统回答哪个问题，绝不能影响返回什么值。生成外壳负责解释模糊输入和表述回复；确定性内核将完全指定的问题与一组有界的可回答问题形态匹配，并通过确定性执行编译为查询。两者在计算任何值之前于用户可读的确认环节汇合，内核无法表达的请求被拒绝而非近似。这种方法被称为结构化弃答，区别于选择性预测和校准置信度的统计弃答——这里的拒绝不需要置信度估计，因为不可回答的请求是不可表示的。论文提供实现无关的规格、五步决策流程，覆盖三个领域，将不变量从返回值扩展到代理系统的动作，并有两年生产案例研究。

Result: 报告了两年生产案例研究，与两种生成式替代方案（微调解析器和工具检索代理）进行对比，并针对其后发布的企业与可靠性基准进行验证。结构化弃答方法使得系统能够在无法确定答案时明确拒绝，而非生成流畅但可能错误的回答，从而在可靠性层面解决了幻觉问题。

Conclusion: 作者的架构模式将"可信"与"生成"分离，通过结构性弃答确保错误的代价从"提供错误值"转变为"拒绝回答"，使拒绝机制不需要依赖置信度估计。这为部署LLM驱动的数据库接口提供了一种可靠性优先的设计范式，适用于人类使用者不检查查询、或使用者是工具代理的企业场景。

Abstract: Large language models have made natural language interfaces to databases (NLIDB) newly credible, but LLM text-to-SQL systems fail in a way that matters for deployment: a hallucinated column or a mis-aggregated total yields a fluent wrong answer, indistinguishable at the point of use from a right one. Where the consumer cannot inspect the generated query, as in enterprise AI deployments and operational dashboards, and increasingly where the consumer is a tool-using agent rather than a person, accuracy alone is insufficient: nothing marks which answers to distrust. This is a reliability problem before it is an accuracy problem.
  We propose an architectural pattern for such systems, a trusted kernel with a generative shell, resting on one invariant: a component that can fabricate may influence which question the system answers, never which value it returns. A generative shell interprets underspecified input and phrases replies; a deterministic kernel matches fully specified questions against a bounded set of answerable question shapes and compiles them to queries by deterministic execution. The two meet at a confirmation the user reads before any value is computed, and requests the kernel cannot express are declined rather than approximated. We call this structural abstention, and distinguish it from the statistical abstention of selective prediction and calibrated confidence: refusal here needs no confidence estimate, because unanswerable requests are unrepresentable.
  We specify the pattern implementation-independently, give a five-decision recipe and work it across three domains, extend the invariant from returned values to the actions of agentic systems, and report a two-year production case study alongside two generative alternatives, a fine-tuned parser and a tool-retrieval agent. We close against enterprise and reliability benchmarks published since.

</details>


### [134] [AI Research Preference Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13940)
*Thomas Simon Foster,Bassel Al Omari,Tingchen Fu,Thomas Mann,Carl Domond,Lucia Cipolina-Kun,Bhavul Gauri,Muna Aghamelu,Alexander D. Goldie,Eryk Helenowski,Jean-Christophe Gagnon-Audet,Alberto Pepe,Saba Nazir,Daniel Izcovich,Noam Levi,Rishi Hazra,Karen Hambardzumyan,Nicolas Baldwin,Xian Li,Martin Josifoski,Paris Giampouras,Masoud Jalili Sabet,Anya Sims,Hela Momand,Tatiana Shavrina,Despoina Magka,Jason Weston,Yulin Wang,Anirudh Goyal,João Henriques,Yoram Bachrach,Emily McMilin,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI研究偏好模型（RPMs），通过预测哪些候选解决方案最值得执行来优化AI研究代理（AIRA）的有限执行预算分配，无需同时执行所有候选方案。RPMs基于冻结的预训练语言模型构建，包含推理模型和代理模型两种形式，集成到AIRA-dojo搜索代理后在AIRS-Bench基准上将平均归一化得分从0.684提升至0.711和0.729，并在约15小时内达到无引导代理24小时的性能，仅使用其三分之二的执行预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI研究代理可以快速提出大量候选机器学习实验方案，但评估每个方案需要耗费数小时到数天的GPU时间，导致候选方案数量远超可执行的预算。因此如何高效分配固定执行预算成为瓶颈，需要一种能够预测候选方案价值而无需全部执行的偏好模型。

Method: 构建AI研究偏好模型（RPMs），基于冻结的预训练语言模型（无任务特定训练），包含两种形式：（1）推理模型——对候选计划、代码及先前已执行方案进行推理排序；（2）代理模型——在执行前额外运行小规模试点实验辅助决策。两种模型均集成到AIRA-dojo搜索代理中。

Result: 在AIRS-Bench基准上，两种RPM变体将平均归一化得分从0.684提升至0.711和0.729；达到无引导代理24小时性能仅需约15小时，消耗不到其三分之二的执行预算；最佳RPM还在两个AIRS-Bench任务上取得了新的最优结果。

Conclusion: RPMs能够有效预测候选解决方案的执行价值，显著提升AI研究代理的执行预算利用效率，在更短时间和更少预算条件下达到甚至超越未引导代理的性能，证明了借助冻结预训练语言模型进行研究偏好建模是一种有效且低成本的折衷方案。

Abstract: AI research agents (AIRA) can now propose, implement, and evaluate their own machine learning experiments, but progress on frontier tasks is throttled by cost: a candidate solution can be written in minutes, whereas evaluating it can take hours to days of GPU time. An agent can therefore propose far more candidates than it can afford to run, and its progress depends on its research preference: how it allocates a fixed execution budget across many candidates. We introduce AI Research Preference Models (RPMs) that predict which of multiple candidate solutions are most worth executing, without paying the cost of executing them all. We build RPMs from frozen pretrained language models (with no task-specific training), in two forms: an inference-only model that reasons over candidate plans, code, and prior executed solutions, and an agentic model that additionally runs small-scale pilot experiments before deciding. We integrate both into the AIRA-dojo search agent and evaluate on AIRS-Bench, a recent benchmark of machine learning research tasks for AI research agents. The two variants raise the average normalized score from 0.684 to 0.711 and 0.729 respectively, and reach the unguided agent's 24-hour performance in roughly 15 hours, using less than two-thirds of its execution budget. Our best RPMs also yield new state-of-the-art results on two AIRS-Bench tasks.

</details>


### [135] [HELIX: Model-Harness Co-evolution for Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13951)
*Tianyu Fan,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HELIX，一个用于智能体系统（harness）进化的可溯源基座。它强调了模型与运行时harness的耦合关系，并实现模型-harness协同进化的递归自改进循环。在代码修复任务上的单轮进化中，65个候选方案发现固定harness使任务覆盖率较Pi提升4.0%，完整组合通过互补兄弟行为多暴露58.0%的已验证覆盖，200槽位的兄弟切片产生438条已验证的SFT、critic、filter和preference训练记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体能力扩展主要聚焦于改进模型本身，但交互式智能体实际通过一个运行时harness（管理上下文、工具、控制流和停止条件）来行动。harness既塑造了模型能完成的任务，也决定了模型学习到的轨迹。这种耦合关系促使需要实现模型与harness的协同进化，以实现递归自改进。

Method: HELIX将智能体系统分解为类型化端口（typed ports）、可复用原子（atoms）、配方（recipes）、产品外壳（product shells）和运行时策略（runtime policies）。它使干预（interventions）显式化且可审计，同时保留轨迹、测试结果和来源（provenance）。进化循环包括：为固定模型构建harness、从已验证的兄弟轨迹更新模型、随模型能力变化重建harness。

Result: 在代码修复任务上，65个候选方案的组合发现一个固定harness使任务覆盖率比Pi提升4.0%；完整组合通过互补的兄弟行为额外暴露58.0%的已验证覆盖。200槽位的兄弟切片产生438条已验证的SFT、critic、filter和preference训练记录。

Conclusion: 结果表明harness、模型和数据构成一个反馈系统：harness进化扩展当前能力并产生下一轮模型的学习信号；模型更新又激励新一轮的harness进化。HELIX为研究这一递归过程提供了可审计的接口。

Abstract: Scaling agent capability has largely focused on improving the model, yet an interactive agent acts through a runtime harness that mediates context, tools, control flow, and stopping. The harness shapes both what a model can accomplish and the trajectories from which it learns. This coupling motivates model-harness co-evolution for recursive self-improvement: build harnesses for a fixed model, update the model from verified sibling trajectories, and rebuild the harnesses as model capabilities change. Realizing this loop requires a controlled way to evolve harnesses while preserving intervention identity and effect. We present HELIX, a source-traceable substrate for harness evolution. HELIX decomposes agent systems into typed ports, reusable atoms, recipes, product shells, and runtime policies. It makes interventions explicit and auditable while retaining trajectories, test outcomes, and provenance. Harness evolution thus serves two linked roles: improving fixed-model execution and producing matched successes, regressions, near misses, and alternative solutions as data for subsequent model improvement. We evaluate HELIX in one evolution round on code repair. A 65-candidate portfolio discovers a fixed harness that improves task coverage by 4.0% over Pi, while the full portfolio exposes up to 58.0% more verified coverage through complementary sibling behavior. Selected candidates are assessed with repeated runs and the SWE-bench evaluator. A 200-slot sibling slice yields 438 verified SFT, critic, filter, and preference records. These results show how harness, model, and data form a feedback system: harness evolution expands current capability and creates learning signal for the next model; model updates motivate the next round of harness evolution. HELIX provides an auditable interface for studying this recursive process. Code is available at https://github.com/HKUDS/HELIX.

</details>


### [136] [Implementing Computational Law in Wolfram Language for the Governance of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13958)
*James K. Wiles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用Reified Input/Output Logic形式化法律规范来治理AI系统，在Wolfram Language中实现了该逻辑核心公理，并测试GPT-4将英文法律陈述翻译为形式化规范的能力，记录了其各类失败模式。通过"AI看门狗"案例展示形式化规则如何从合同直接延伸到具身智能体的运行代码中，产生可审计的符号化行为理由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统的推理过程无法被完全审查，但治理不要求理解系统推理，只需明确系统的义务、允许和禁止行为并检查其合规性。作者旨在证明计算法（computational law）可以作为AI治理工具。

Method: 在Wolfram Language中实现了Reified Input/Output Logic形式化系统（包含I/O核心公理、义务、许可、构成性规范、具体化事件和时态算子），并使用GPT-4测试将英文法律陈述自动翻译为该形式化系统的能力。通过案例研究——受计算合同约束的AI看门狗——展示形式化规则如何从合同扩展到具身智能体的运行代码。

Result: GPT-4翻译存在多种失败模式：幻觉函数、遗漏时间范围、偏离形式化框架，最严重的是代码能运行且看似合理但静默编码了错误规范。案例分析表明形式化规则可从合同直接延伸到具身智能体的操作代码中，产生符号化、可审计的行为理由。

Conclusion: 计算法可以用作AI治理工具，一个理想目标是形式化那些可以且应当以程序方式执行的法律规范。

Abstract: How do we govern AI systems whose reasoning we cannot fully inspect? Governance does not require understanding a system's reasoning. It requires stating what the system is obliged, permitted, and forbidden to do, and checking whether it complied. I present an implementation of Reified Input/Output Logic, the formalism behind the DAPRECO knowledge base, in Wolfram Language: the core I/O axioms, obligations, permissions, constitutive norms, reified eventualities, and temporal operators. I then test whether GPT-4 can translate English legal statements into the formalism, and report the failures: hallucinated functions, omitted temporal scope, deviation from the formalism, and (in the worst cases) code that runs, reads plausibly, but silently encodes the wrong norm. A case study, an AI guard dog operating under a computational contract, shows how formalized rules can extend from a contract directly into the operational code of an embodied agent, producing symbolic, auditable justifications for its behaviour. I argue that computational law can be used as a governance tool and that a desirable goal would be to formalize the law that can and ought to be programmatically executable.

</details>


### [137] [Nanbeige4.2-3B on Apple Silicon: Fixing Deployment Bugs and Decreasing Looped Transformer Memory Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13987)
*John T. Halloran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对3B参数规模的agentic模型Nanbeige4.2-3B进行评测与修复，发现其在Apple Silicon (MPS)上存在五个独立bug导致无法开箱即用，修复后引入chunked-prefill策略将上下文宽度扩展2.7倍，最终使模型在MCPMark上完成30%的真实agentic任务（原始为0%），并在BFCL上单工具调用近乎完美但多工具测试大多失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并修复Nanbeige4.2-3B——一个基于Looped Transformer (LT)的3B参数agentic模型，其在Apple Silicon (MPS)上无法通过Hugging Face transformers直接运行的问题，并评估其修复后是否真正可用于agentic任务。

Method: 1) 系统性地识别并修复五个独立bug，包括静默清零的RoPE缓冲区和对已移除transformers缓存API的调用；2) 分析LT层复用策略导致的峰值注意力内存翻倍问题，提出chunked-prefill策略缓解内存容量惩罚；3) 进一步修复系统提示词和MPS原生内存bug；4) 在MCPMark和BFCL标准基准上进行评测。

Result: chunked-prefill策略在32 GiB共享内存上将上下文宽度扩展2.7倍；修复后的模型在MCPMark子集上可完成高达30%的真实agentic任务（原始为0%）；在BFCL上单工具调用近乎完美，但多工具测试大部分失败。

Conclusion: Nanbeige4.2-3B通过Looped Transformer实现参数效率，但层复用策略带来了显著的内存惩罚，即使修复所有bug并引入chunked-prefill，模型仍需要在系统提示词和MPS内存方面进一步打补丁才能可靠使用，且多工具调用能力仍是一大短板。

Abstract: Nanbeige4.2-3B is a 3B-parameter agentic model built around a Looped Transformer (LT) that reuses one stack of layers for a second forward pass, adding effective depth without additional parameters. Evaluated on Apple Silicon (MPS), we identify five independent bugs which prevent the released checkpoint from running via Hugging Face transformers out of the box (including a silently-zeroed RoPE buffer and calls to removed transformers cache APIs). Furthermore, we show that fixing these bugs is still not sufficient for agentic tasks, due to the LT's layer-reuse strategy (which effectively doubles peak attention memory) used to achieve parameter efficiency. We thus introduce a chunked-prefill strategy which alleviates the incurred memory-capacity penalty, extending allowable context width by $2.7 \times$ on 32~GiB shared memory. However, even with the reduced memory overhead, we show that patches are required to render Nanbeige4.2-3B usable; resolving both system prompt and MPS-native memory bugs finally allows reliable evaluation on standard MCP and tool-calling benchmarks. On a subset of MCPMark, the debugged model completes up to 30\% of real agentic tasks (up from the original's 0\%), while, on BFCL, it is near-perfect at single tool calls (yet fails the majority of multi-tool tests). We release the patched checkpoint, system prompt optimizer, and evaluation harnesses at https://github.com/johnhalloran321/Nanbeige4.2-3B-mps-fix.

</details>


### [138] [Content Depth Matters in Short-Video Recommendation: Rethinking the Attention Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13990)
*Liwei Deng,Jing Jiang,Zhiwei Li,Yang Wang,Guodong Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出内容深度分数（CDS）用于量化短视频的内容深度，并构建了首个面向短视频推荐的内容深度评估基准SCOPE-Bench（含15万条视频的CDS标注）。基于该基准评估了13个代表性推荐系统，发现它们一致偏好浅层内容视频，且对深度内容推荐效果仅略优于随机选择，揭示出现有推荐目标中此前被忽视的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在注意力经济驱动下，短视频推荐系统以最大化用户参与度为目标，倾向于推荐能在数秒内吸引注意力的浅层内容视频。然而有证据表明长期接触此类内容可能对用户的认知投入和心理健康产生负面影响，引发对短视频平台长期社会影响的担忧。现有推荐系统缺乏对内容深度的量化评估手段。

Method: (1) 提出内容深度分数（CDS）指标，基于认知心理学和学习理论，采用七级量表衡量视频激发高阶认知过程（如分析、综合、评价等）的程度；(2) 构建SCOPE-Bench基准，基于大规模开源短视频数据集，为15万条视频提供CDS标注；(3) 利用SCOPE-Bench系统评估13个代表性推荐系统，从认知内容维度考察其推荐质量。

Result: 实验发现13个代表性推荐系统一致表现出对浅层内容视频的偏好；这些算法在推荐认知深度内容方面的表现仅略优于随机选择。这揭示出现有推荐目标函数在内容深度维度上存在此前被忽视的严重局限。

Conclusion: 该研究为短视频推荐的内容深度评估提供了首个标准化基准与量化框架，指出引导推荐系统兼顾内容深度、减少对浅层内容过度偏好的必要性，为构建更具认知友好性和社会可持续性的推荐系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Driven by the attention economy, short-video Recommender Systems (RSs) are primarily optimized to maximize user engagement by promoting videos that capture attention within seconds. These systems inherently favor shallow-content videos that are effective at attracting immediate attention. However, growing evidence suggests that prolonged exposure to such content may negatively affect users' cognitive engagement and mental well-being, raising concerns about the long-term societal impact of the short-video platform. To tackle this challenge, this paper introduces a new metric, the \textbf{Content Depth Score (CDS)}, to quantify the content depth of short videos. CDS measures the extent to which a video is expected to stimulate higher-order cognitive processes, using a seven-level scale grounded in established theories of cognitive psychology and learning. As an initial step toward this vision, we present \textbf{SCOPE-Bench}, the first benchmark for content-depth evaluation in short-video recommendation. Built upon a large-scale open-source short-video dataset, SCOPE-Bench provides CDS annotations for 150K videos, enabling systematic evaluation of RSs from a cognitive-content perspective. Leveraging SCOPE-Bench, we evaluate 13 representative RSs and reveal a consistent preference for shallow-content videos. Moreover, we find that these algorithms recommending cognitively deep content are only marginally better than random selection, highlighting a previously overlooked limitation of existing recommendation objectives. Our code and datasets are available at https://liweidengdavid.github.io/SCOPE-Bench/.

</details>


### [139] [Simulation-Driven Vehicular Traffic Data Augmentation: Extending Sensor Coverage Through Virtual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13993)
*Davide Andrea Guastella,Eladio Montero Porras,Evangelos Pournaras,Gianluca Bontempi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于仿真的数据增强方法，通过在路网中引入虚拟传感器来扩充交通计数数据集，从而解决因传感器部署成本和隐私限制导致的稀疏覆盖及模型泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通管理依赖传感器网络，但部署成本与隐私法规限制了空间覆盖范围。基于此类稀疏数据训练的机器学习模型无法泛化到未监测位置，且传感器基础设施一旦变化就需要重新训练模型。

Method: 通过仿真生成增强的交通计数数据集：将每个物理传感器替换为放置于路网替代位置的虚拟传感器。虚拟传感器通过图搜索启发式方法选取，该方法联合最大化车辆流连续性和原位置与替代位置之间的交通指标相似性，同时强制最小空间位移以保证所观测交通状况的多样性。

Result: 方法在两个比利时城市得到验证：布鲁塞尔（使用校准模型）和那慕尔（使用合成模型）。增强后的数据集保留了观测位置的双峰日需求特征和交通动态。

Conclusion: 所提出的仿真驱动数据增强方法能够在维持原始交通模式特征的前提下扩充传感器数据覆盖范围，缓解稀疏传感器网络带来的模型泛化与重训练问题。

Abstract: Urban traffic management relies on sensor networks whose spatial coverage is limited by deployment costs and privacy regulations. Machine learning models trained on such sparse data cannot generalize to unmonitored locations and must be retrained whenever the sensor infrastructure changes. We propose a simulation-based methodology that addresses this problem by generating augmented traffic count datasets in which each physical sensor is replaced by a virtual sensor placed at a surrogate location in the road network. Virtual sensors are selected by a graph-search heuristic that jointly maximises vehicle-flow continuity and traffic-metric similarity between the original and surrogate locations, while enforcing a minimum spatial displacement to ensure diversity of observed traffic conditions. We validate the method on two Belgian cities: Brussels, using a calibrated model, and Namur, using synthetic models. The augmented datasets preserve the bimodal daily demand profile and the dynamics of traffic at the observed locations.

</details>


### [140] [Buy the Rumor, Sell the News: When Is News Priced In?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14014)
*Alireza Kargarzadeh,Nariman Khaledian,Navid Parvini,Sid Ghatak,Arman Khaledian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于2023-2026年间4.57百万篇金融新闻（覆盖约3000只美股）构建大规模实证研究，使用大语言模型教师蒸馏出紧凑分类器对新闻进行标注，将文章聚类为故事以区分首发报道与后续报道，测量1.68百万个股票-日事件周围的beta调整异常收益。研究发现：价格变动集中在发布日及之前（发布日收盘累计变动是20天后的2.8倍）；市场对量化基本面新闻（盈利、分红、指引、分析师行动）反应不足而产生持续漂移，对软性故事型新闻（发布、宏观评论、领导层变动）反应过度而回吐涨幅；新闻不仅带来方向性影响还带来波动率抬升（发布前波动加剧、发布后回落）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验两条经典市场谚语——"新闻发布时价格已消化完毕"和"谣言时买入、新闻时卖出"是否成立，以及它们适用于哪些类型的新闻、程度如何。这些是理解市场吸收公共信息速度的基本问题。

Method: 使用大语言模型教师通过主动学习蒸馏得到紧凑分类器，为每篇新闻标注17种事件标签和5种属性；将文章聚合成故事以区分首次报道与后续报道；对1.68百万个股票-日事件测量beta调整的异常收益，并以364,405个中性情绪事件作为安慰剂对照组。

Result: 三项核心发现：（1）价格变动集中在发布前和发布日，发布日累计变动为20天后的2.8倍；谣言标志事件中谣言日捕获全部变动，后续确认无额外贡献。（2）与安慰剂对照组相比，市场对量化基本面新闻反应不足（收益持续漂移数周），对软性故事新闻反应过度（收益回吐）。（3）新闻具有宽度效应：发布前波动率上升，发布后波动率下降，因为发布解决了不确定性。

Conclusion: 实证支持新闻价格效应主要在发布前及发布时刻而非发布后显现；不同类型新闻的市场反应模式存在系统差异（基本面新闻漂移、故事型新闻反转）；新闻同时影响收益方向和波动率水平。研究还产出了各事件标签的漂移系数表，可作为新闻条件预测模型的先验知识。

Abstract: Two old market sayings hold that news is already priced in by the time it is published, and that the rumor is bought while the news is sold. Both place the price move associated with a piece of news before and at publication rather than after it. Whether the claims hold, for which kinds of news, and by how much are basic questions about how fast markets absorb public information. We test them on 4.57 million financial news articles covering roughly 3,000 US stocks (2023-2026). A large language model teacher, distilled into a compact classifier through active learning, assigns each article one of 17 event tags and five attributes; articles are clustered into stories to separate first reports from follow-up coverage; and beta-adjusted abnormal returns are measured around the resulting 1.68 million stock-day events, with 364,405 neutral-sentiment events as a placebo group. Three results follow. First, the price move associated with news concentrates before and at publication: pooled across all signed events, the cumulative move in the news direction by the close of publication day is 2.8 times its value 20 days later, and for rumor-flagged events the rumor day captures the entire move while the subsequent confirmation contributes nothing. Second, measured against the placebo of comparable stocks, markets underreact to numbers and overreact to stories: quantified fundamental news (earnings, dividends, guidance, analyst actions) keeps drifting in the direction of the news for weeks, while soft story-driven news (launches, macro commentary, leadership) gives back its move. Third, news carries width as well as direction: publicity raises volatility before the publication day, and volatility declines once the news is out, because publication resolves uncertainty. The study also produces a table of measured drift for each event tag, usable as a prior in news-conditioned forecasting models.

</details>


### [141] [Residual Dominance as a Structural Account of Last-Item Reliance in Causal Self-Attention Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14021)
*Keito Kozaki,Keigo Sakurai,Ren Togo,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过推理时诊断和基于范数的注意力块分析，揭示了基于Transformer的序列推荐器中极端的最后一项依赖（last-item reliance）在结构上是由残差连接主导（residual dominance）造成的。通过推理时残差缩放干预，作者发现残差强度与结构混合程度及最后一项依赖之间存在单调权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的序列推荐器（如SASRec）在推理时高度依赖最近一次交互，但这种行为如何在预测所使用的表示中结构化地表达仍不清楚。作者旨在从结构层面解释这一现象，而不仅仅停留在行为层面的观察。

Method: 1) 将预测时诊断（prediction-time diagnostics）与基于范数的全注意力块分析相结合；2) 通过分析残差加法对全块表示的影响，提出"残差主导"（residual dominance）概念；3) 使用推理时残差缩放（inference-time residual scaling）作为受控诊断干预来验证解释。

Result: 1) SASRec类模型表现出高度局部化的最后一项依赖；2) 尽管自注意力聚合了上下文信息，残差加法使全块表示显著偏向同位置贡献（残差主导）；3) 改变残差强度会在结构混合与最后一项依赖之间产生单调权衡；4) 降低残差强度可恢复一部分末尾位置预测失败，这些情况下非末尾位置的表征已能正确排序目标物品。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个结构性解释，将极端的最后一项依赖与推理时残差主导现象联系起来，深化了对Transformer序列推荐器内部机制的理解。

Abstract: Transformer-based sequential recommenders with causal self-attention often rely heavily on the most recent interaction at inference time, but how this behavior is structurally expressed in the representation used for prediction remains unclear. We combine prediction-time diagnostics with norm-based analysis of the full attention block. First, we show that SASRec-style models exhibit highly localized last-item reliance. We then find that, although self-attention aggregates contextual information, residual addition sharply shifts the full-block representation toward same-position contributions, which we term residual dominance. To probe this interpretation, we use inference-time residual scaling as a controlled diagnostic intervention. Changing the residual strength induces a monotonic trade-off between structural mixing and last-item reliance, while reducing residual strength recovers a subset of final-position misses for which representations at non-final positions already rank the ground-truth item correctly. Our results provide a structural account linking extreme last-item reliance to residual dominance at inference time. The code is publicly available.

</details>


### [142] [Agent-Orchestration in Autonomous Chip Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14035)
*Linyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将芯片设计超级智能（Chip-Design Superintelligence）建模为一种庞大的"AI组织"，探讨大型语言模型和工具使用智能体在芯片设计行业中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来LLM和工具型智能体的快速发展推动了智能体在芯片设计中的应用探索，但核心问题在于这个复杂精密的行业究竟需要什么样的AI。

Method: 文章提出将芯片设计超级智能建模为一个由众多AI智能体组成的大型组织（AI-organization），以组织化的方式协同完成芯片设计任务。

Result: 该摘要未给出具体实验结果，主要提出概念框架和思考方向。

Conclusion: 芯片设计领域的AI发展方向应从单一智能体转向大规模AI组织化的超级智能形态。

Abstract: Recent developments in large language models (LLMs) and tool-using agents encourage people to explore the potential of using agents in chip design. The core question is what kind of AI we really need in such a sophisticated industry.
  To this end, we bring the idea of modeling a chip-design superintelligence as an enormous \textit{AI-organization}.

</details>


### [143] [Demystifying Agent Skills: Why They Work-Until They Don't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14036)
*Zhiyuan Jiang,Fangrui Huang,Hanwen Xing,Xander Wu,Yipeng Gao,Rui Cao,Mengdi Wang,Shilong Liu,Yijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验与配对轨迹分析，系统研究了技能（skills）在增强LLM智能体时的作用机制，提出了三类十二种的技能使用模式分类法，发现技能主要在轨迹噪声大时作为"程序性锚点"稳定执行（占65.7%），而非注入缺失的事实知识；同时揭示了检索是独立瓶颈，并界定了技能失效的三种条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估大多只衡量技能是否提升聚合任务成功率，而忽略了更根本的问题：技能何时起效、为何起效、何处失效。作者旨在超越聚合成功率的评估范式，深入理解技能机制，为可靠的自进化智能体提供指导。

Method: 采用对比研究方法，结合受控定量实验与配对轨迹分析。在多种基准、agent框架和LLM上进行受控实验，隔离表示方式、结果标注、检索难度和跨框架鲁棒性等因素的影响；对8,135条受控实验试次记录进行归一化处理，并从240条开放式编码记录中保留238条有效标签，归纳出三类十二种技能使用模式的分类体系。

Result: 技能在噪声轨迹中作为程序性锚点稳定执行，匹配比较中比Workflow Memory提升6.06分。程序性锚定占技能使用场景的65.7%，而显式知识注入仅占4.5%。检索是独立瓶颈：池从5增至100时，实际使用精确率从29.6%降至3.3%。混淆干扰项损害离线识别但下游成功率保持稳定；精确的地面真值调用既非充分也非必要条件。技能在脆弱假设、不兼容上下文或适应不足时失败。

Conclusion: 技能的核心价值在于稳定智能体的动作执行而非补充知识，检索效率与技能质量是相互独立的瓶颈。该研究将评估从聚合成功率拓展到对技能机制的理解，为设计和部署可靠的自我进化智能体提供了实证依据和分类指导。

Abstract: Skills have emerged as a practical and effective approach for enhancing LLM agents at inference time through structured packages of knowledge. However, existing evaluations largely measure whether skills improve aggregated task success, leaving a more fundamental question underexplored: \emph{\textbf{When do skills help, why do they work, and where do they fail?}} Through controlled experiments across various benchmarks, agent harnesses and LLMs, we isolate the effects of representation, outcome annotation, retrieval difficulty, and cross-framework robustness of skills. To further answer this question, we design a contrastive study that combines controlled quantitative experiments with paired trajectory analysis. We normalize 8,135 trial records from controlled experiments and retain 238 valid unique labels from 240 open-coded records. We consolidate these observations into a taxonomy of three high-level categories and twelve skill-use modes: skills work when noisy trajectories become procedural anchors that stabilize execution. Skills improve over Workflow Memory by 6.06 points in matched comparisons. Procedural anchoring accounts for 65.7\% of skill cases, versus 4.5\% for explicit knowledge injection, showing that skills stabilize action rather than inject missing facts. Retrieval is a separate bottleneck: as pools grow from 5 to 100, actual-use precision falls from 29.6\% to 3.3\%. Confusable distractors impair offline identification, yet downstream success remains stable; exact ground-truth invocation is neither sufficient nor necessary. Skills fail under brittle assumptions, incompatible contexts, or insufficient adaptation. These findings move evaluation beyond aggregate success rates and guide reliable self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [144] [Benchmarking data-driven material models on the classic Treloar dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14063)
*Hagen Holthusen,Moritz Flaschel,Denisa Martonová,Ellen Kuhl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用Treloar经典实验数据，对六种机器学习驱动的超弹性本构建模方法进行了系统性基准测试，比较了拟合性能、计算成本、超参数敏感性和实现难易度，并讨论了预测精度与模型复杂度之间的权衡。结果显示所有方法都能很好地复现基准数据，但各有优缺点，作者为每种方法的适用场景提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于机器学习的本构建模方法数量快速增长，研究者面临选择困难：不同方法在实践中的相对表现如何、各自的优缺点是什么、该如何选择合适的方法，目前缺乏系统性的横向对比研究。

Method: 以Treloar经典实验数据为统一基准，对六种流行方法进行系统基准测试：(广义不变量)本构人工神经网络、物理增强神经网络、(自适应)材料指纹识别、以及高效无监督本构定律识别与发现。从四个维度评估：拟合性能、计算成本、超参数敏感性、实现难易度；同时通过材料参数数量和本构模型及其导数的计算时间两个指标量化模型复杂度。

Result: 所有六种方法均能在Treloar基准数据上取得出色的拟合效果。研究未判定单一"赢家"，而是揭示每种方法各自的优势与局限，例如不同方法在拟合精度与模型复杂度之间存在不同的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 结论是该领域不存在普适的最优方法，研究者应根据具体应用需求（如精度要求、计算资源、实现成本等）选择合适的方法。论文提供了每种方法的实践使用指南，并公开了全部源代码、训练/对比脚本、结果和数据以促进可复现性和后续比较。

Abstract: Machine learning is rapidly reshaping constitutive modeling, offers new ways to learn material behavior directly from experimental data, and challenges long-established modeling paradigms. But with a growing number of machine-learning-based approaches available, how do they compare in practice? In this paper, we use the classic experimental data of Treloar to benchmark popular frameworks for hyperelasticity: (Generalized-Invariant) Constitutive Artificial Neural Networks, Physics-Augmented Neural Networks, (Adaptive) Material Fingerprinting, and Efficient Unsupervised Constitutive Law Identification & Discovery. We compare their fitting performance, computational cost, hyperparameter sensitivity, and ease of implementation. Furthermore, we discuss the trade-offs between predictive accuracy and model complexity. The latter is assessed by quantifying both the number of material parameters in the discovered models and the computational time required to evaluate the constitutive model and its derivatives. The results show that all methods can reproduce the benchmark data remarkably well. Rather than identifying a single winner, we highlight the strengths and limitations of each approach and provide practical guidance for their use. The source code for all six methods, including the training and comparison scripts, as well as all results and data used in this study, is publicly available via https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21915635.

</details>


### [145] [Regime-Conditional Verification: Correctness Estimation for Adapting and Monitoring Safety Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14089)
*Thiago Sandoval,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Regime-Conditional Verification (RCV)，一种轻量级包装器，利用安全分类器的内部表示估计预测与部署者策略不一致的概率，并有选择性地纠正可能错误的预测。该正确性估计同时提供无标签分布漂移检测信号，形成维护闭环。在三种现成安全分类器和两个基准数据集上，RCV在每个组合中都提升了策略遵循度，最多捕获0.81的此前遗漏的不安全内容，且无需修改底层分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全分类器存在两个核心缺陷：其决策反映的是训练时学到的策略而非部署者期望的策略；且随部署流量演化，分类器性能会退化。这导致分类器在面对新攻击和演化分布时失效，需要一种无需重训即可适配部署者策略并应对分布漂移的轻量级方案。

Method: RCV从分类器的内部表示中估计每个预测与部署者策略不一致的概率，并选择性纠正高风险的预测。该正确性估计被复用作无标签分布漂移检测信号，触发维护循环：先更新正确性估计层进行修复，仅当修复无法恢复时才进行分类器微调。

Result: 在三种现成安全分类器和两个基准数据集上，RCV在所有组合中均提升了策略遵循度，最多捕获0.81的此前遗漏的不安全内容。在十种攻击场景的部署研究中，RCV在专用注入面板中检测到每个攻击场景；维护普查中多数漂移事件无需更新分类器即可修复，微调仅保留给修复无法恢复的残余事件。

Conclusion: RCV证明了对部署者策略的适配和分布漂移的应对可以在不重训底层分类器的情况下实现，通过选择性纠正和分层维护机制有效提升了安全分类器的实际部署适应性。

Abstract: Safety classifiers deployed with large language models often fail for two reasons: their decisions reflect the policy learned during training rather than the deployer's desired policy, and their performance degrades as deployment traffic evolves. We present Regime-Conditional Verification (RCV), a lightweight wrapper that adapts an off-the-shelf safety classifier without retraining it. RCV estimates, from the classifier's internal representations, the probability that each prediction disagrees with the deployer's policy, and selectively corrects predictions likely to be wrong. The same correctness estimates also provide a label-free signal for detecting distribution shift, enabling a maintenance loop that updates the correctness estimation layer and resorts to classifier fine-tuning only when necessary. Across three off-the-shelf safety classifiers and two benchmark datasets, RCV improves adherence to the deployer's policy in every classifier-dataset combination, catching up to 0.81 of previously missed unsafe content without modifying the underlying classifier. In a deployment study with ten attack campaigns, each a harm category held out of RCV's training, RCV detects every campaign in a dedicated injection panel; in the maintenance census most drift episodes are repaired without updating the classifier, and the fine-tune is reserved for the residual episodes that repair does not restore.

</details>


### [146] [Scaling Domain Data Repetition in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14071)
*Jingwei Li,Xinran Gu,Rui Dai,Xintong Hao,Chengyin Xu,Yan Wu,Shuran Zheng,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型扩展过程中高质量领域数据的重复使用策略。研究发现，在固定tokens-per-parameter（TPP）比率下，最优重复次数随模型规模轻微增加；不同领域间，最优重复次数与最终验证损失呈强负相关（损失越低可重复越多），但与领域数据总量关系较弱。基于此，作者提出可用较小代理模型在同TPP下调优重复次数来估算大模型的最优重复设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型规模扩大，训练token预算需同步增长以维持合适的TPP比率。但高质量领域数据难以像通用网络数据那样规模化扩展，其在训练混合中的占比趋于下降。重复使用现有高质量数据是抵消这种稀释的有效手段，但过度重复会导致过拟合。因此需要在重复利用与过拟合之间找到最优平衡点，该问题在真实LLM扩展场景下尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在训练token预算随模型规模成比例增长的实用LLM扩展设定下，针对固定领域，分析在不同模型规模下最优重复次数的变化规律；同时跨领域分析最优重复次数与最终验证损失、唯一领域数据量之间的相关性，并据此评估小规模代理模型的迁移性。

Result: 发现一：在固定TPP下，最优重复次数随模型规模温和增加（出乎意料）；发现二：跨领域中，最优重复次数与最终验证损失呈强负相关，即损失越低的领域可受益于更多重复；发现三：唯一领域数据量仅与最优重复次数存在弱相关。由此得出，在相同TPP下用较小代理模型调优的重复次数可作为大模型的实用估算。

Conclusion: 领域数据的最优重复次数主要由领域本身的验证损失（即可学习性/难度）而非数据量决定，且这一规律在不同模型规模间具有可迁移性。这为大规模LLM训练中高质量领域数据的重复策略提供了实用的调参指导：可通过同TPP的小模型实验来预估大模型的最优重复次数。

Abstract: As large language models scale, their training-token budgets must also increase to maintain an appropriate tokens-per-parameter ratio (\(\mathrm{TPP}\)). However, high-quality domain data is much harder to scale than general web data. As model size and the training-token budget increase, its fraction in the training mixture tends to decrease. Repeating the available high-quality data provides an effective way to counteract this dilution, but excessive repetition may lead to overfitting. We study this trade-off under practical LLM scaling, where the training-token budget grows proportionally with model size. For a fixed domain, we first find that, surprisingly at a fixed \(\mathrm{TPP}\), the optimal repetition count mildly increases with model size. Across different domains, we find that the optimal repetition count is strongly negatively correlated with the final validation loss of a domain: domains with lower loss can generally benefit from more repetitions. In contrast, the amount of unique domain data is only weakly related to the optimal repetition count. These findings suggest that repetition counts tuned on smaller proxy models with the same \(\mathrm{TPP}\) can provide a practical estimate for larger models.

</details>


### [147] [Mandato: Protocol-Level Enforcement of Digitally Signed Mandates on AI Agent Actions with Cryptographically Chained Audit Trails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14074)
*Giovanni Racioppi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mandato 是一个协议层的治理代理（governance proxy），通过在 MCP（Model Context Protocol）等标准化工具调用协议上强制实施数字签名的"授权令（mandate）"，对 AI 代理的工具调用行为进行可验证的授权约束。该系统将授权逻辑从应用代码中抽离，以机器可读、密码学签名的形式定义代理可以调用哪些工具、在何种参数约束与上下文条件下、持续多久、代表谁行事，并在线阻止不合规调用，同时生成追加式（append-only）、哈希链式（hash-chained）的审计日志以供证据用途，并定期通过合格时间戳（qualified timestamps）锚定。Mandato 有意借鉴民法中"授权委托"制度，使审计人员和律师而非仅工程师也能理解该工件，并映射到 EU AI Act 第12/14条、GDPR、NIS2 与 eIDAS 2 等法规，提出通过合格信任服务提供商（QTSP）实现合格认证的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 代理越来越多地通过 MCP 等标准化协议与外部系统交互，但缺乏基础设施层来约束其行为必须严格限于委托人已可验证授权的范围。现有的授权逻辑散落在应用代码中，既没有签名也不可独立审计，产生的日志缺乏证据价值。这构成了一个关键安全与治理空白：无法从技术层面保证代理行为被限制在授权范围内，也无法提供具有法律效力的审计证据。

Method: 作者设计了一个名为 Mandato 的治理代理，其核心创新在于"授权令（mandate）"这一密码学签名的机器可读授权工件。授权令明确指定代理可调用的工具、参数约束、上下文条件、有效期及被代理方。Mandato 作为 MCP 透明的中间代理，将决策点与执行点分离，每个工具调用都会对照适用的授权令链进行评估，在线阻止不合规调用，并将每次决策（允许、拒绝及依据）记录在追加式、哈希链式的审计日志中。作者还基于民法授权委托制度构建了授权令模型，并提供 EU AI Act 等多法规映射与 QTSP 合格认证路线图。

Result: 论文给出了授权令的形式化模型与决策语义、作为 MCP 透明代理并分离决策/执行点的参考架构、以及对 EU AI Act 第12/14条、GDPR 问责、NIS2 和 eIDAS 2 的法规映射，提出了通过 QTSP 实现合格认证的路线图。此外还报告了参考系统的实现状态，并制定了涵盖执行开销、审计完整性和篡改证据验证成本的定量评估计划。

Conclusion: Mandato 的核心主张是：可以在协议层面（而非应用层面）对 AI 代理的工具调用实施可验证、可审计、具有法律证据效力的授权约束。通过将授权从应用代码中抽离为签名工件，并用因果链审计日志记录决策证据，它为 AI 代理治理提供了兼具技术严谨性与法律合规性的基础设施，为后续实证评估和合规落地（尤其是欧盟法规背景）奠定了基础。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act on external systems through standardized tool-calling protocols such as the Model Context Protocol (MCP), yet no infrastructure layer constrains their actions to what a principal has verifiably authorized: authorization logic lives in application code, is neither signed nor independently auditable, and the resulting logs lack evidentiary value. We present Mandato, a governance proxy that enforces digitally signed mandates on agent actions at the protocol level. A mandate is a machine-readable, cryptographically signed authorization artifact specifying which tools an agent may invoke, under which parameter constraints and contextual conditions, for how long, and on whose behalf; the proxy evaluates every tool call against the applicable mandate chain, blocks non-conforming calls in line, and records every decision -- permit, deny, and the evidence for each -- in an append-only, hash-chained audit log designed for evidentiary use and periodically anchored via qualified timestamps. The mandate is deliberately modeled on the civil-law institution of delegation of authority, making the artifact legible to lawyers and auditors, not only to engineers. We give the mandate model and its decision semantics, the reference architecture as an MCP-transparent proxy with separated decision and enforcement points, and a mapping of the mechanism onto EU AI Act Articles 12 and 14, GDPR accountability, NIS2, and eIDAS 2, including a roadmap to qualified attestation through Qualified Trust Service Providers (QTSPs). We describe the implementation status of the reference system and a quantitative evaluation plan covering enforcement overhead, audit completeness, and tamper-evidence verification cost.

</details>


### [148] [A Pathway to General-Purpose Scientific AI: Multimodal Comprehension of Scientific Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14075)
*Jennifer D'Souza,Fahad Ahmed,Cecilia Andrea Bustamante Andrade,Lina Frolova,Poorani Gnanasambandan,Dilshad Hussain,Muhammad Uzair Khan,Nkembeng Kevin Nkengfoa,Paul Praveen J.,Fabio Priante,Sjoerd Franciscus van der Werf,Thomas Frederik Jan van Roeden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文概述了ALD/E-ImageMiner基准与ICDAR 2026科学图表信息提取竞赛，包含1,951张科学图表，并提出了"科学概念图像理解"这一长期基准目标，展望了多模态科学AI的未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表承载关键实验证据，但数字图书馆和多模态AI系统在检索和解释这些图表方面仍面临重大挑战，亟需标准化基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: 构建了包含1,951张图表、205篇出版物的基准数据集，由专家标注四个任务（分类、数据表提取、摘要生成、视觉问答），并基于Bloom教育分类法设计问题以支撑更深层的科学理解。

Result: 提出了"科学概念图像理解"作为长期基准目标，并规划了未来方向：更广泛的领域与图表类型、跨文档综合、假设评估、溯源、不确定性、反事实推理及开放式多模态研究。
