Furthermore, comprehensive comparisons with zero-shot and fine-tuned foundation models show that RAEF achieves competitive or superior performance to fine-tuning while avoiding its computational burden, establishing it as a practical and scalable approach for domain adaptation in time series forecasting.

</details>


### [250] [When Denoising Hurts: Rethinking the Terminal Step of Diffusion Time Series Forecasters -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14067)
*Dat Nguyen-Cong,Luong Tran,Tung Kieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑扩散模型在时间序列预测中"迭代采样全流程有益"的假设，发现高频噪声阶段已能恢复一般时间结构，而低噪声持续细化会产生统计漂移并降低预测质量。据此提出无标签全局停止准则以检测最优终止点，并设计贝努利时间步采样器聚焦于高噪声区域训练。在8个真实数据集上验证了方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作通常将扩散模型的迭代采样视为一个均匀有益的细化过程，但作者质疑这一观点，认为并非所有逆扩散步骤都对预测质量有贡献，低噪声区域的持续细化反而可能引入统计漂移、降低最终预测效果，因此需要重新审视逆扩散过程中的预测质量演化规律。

Method: 1) 通过实证分析揭示一般时间结构在高噪声水平下即可恢复，低噪声细化反而引入统计漂移；2) 提出一个无需标签的全局停止准则，用于检测逆扩散过程中的最优终止点；3) 提出贝努利时间步采样器，在训练时集中采样于高噪声区域，同时保持对整个扩散过程的覆盖。

Result: 在八个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的方法在推理加速和预测精度两方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在时间序列预测中并非所有采样步骤都是有益的，提前停止不仅能加速推理，还能提升预测精度；针对高噪声区域设计的训练采样策略能进一步强化这一优势。

Abstract: Diffusion models offer a natural way to model uncertainty in time series forecasting, yet their iterative sampling process is often treated as a uniformly beneficial refinement procedure. Our study challenges this view by examining how forecast quality evolves throughout reverse diffusion. We find that general temporal structure is often recovered at relatively high noise levels, whereas continued low-noise refinement can introduce statistical drift and degrade the final forecast. Our analysis further suggests that this behavior explains why prior methods often favor relatively narrow diffusion architecture and schedule design. Building on this observation, we propose a label-free global stopping criterion that detects the optimal termination point, eventually speeding up inference and improving predictive accuracy. Additionally, since early stopping terminates inference in high-noise regions, we propose a Bernoulli timestep sampler that concentrates training on this region while preserving coverage of the full diffusion process. Extensive experiments conducted across eight real-world datasets demonstrate the superior performance of our method compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [251] [Resource-Adaptive Primal-Dual Learning for One-Warehouse Multi-Store Systems with Censored Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14096)
*Jiameng Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出资源自适应原始对偶学习（Resource-Adaptive Primal-Dual Learning）框架，用于单仓库多门店（OWMS）库存系统。该框架突破传统固定目标分配机制的局限，通过追踪随剩余资源状态演化的原始-对偶求解路径，实现了对数级期望遗憾，优于现有平方根级保障，并辅以数值实验验证了有限时域性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OWMS学习策略围绕固定目标设计，该目标校准于初始平均资源率。然而，当实际销售发生改变后，剩余资源对未来各期的可用量会发生变化，固定目标架构无法重新居中调整，导致性能次优。因此需要一种能够随剩余资源状态动态调整的在线学习框架。

Method: 资源自适应原始对偶学习框架：(1) 以当前资源率索引目标门店分配和对偶变量，追踪原始对偶求解路径；(2) 利用审查销售数据（censored sales）提供梯度估计，同时更新资源分配和对偶变量；(3) 将期望销售几何性质与移动目标论证相结合进行分析。

Result: 理论结果显示对数级期望遗憾（logarithmic expected regret），显著优于现有OWMS学习策略的平方根级保障；数值实验在不同时域长度和库存制度下展示了实际变体良好的有限时域性能。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为OWMS系统提供了更优的理论保证，其设计思想（动态跟踪资源状态、实时调整分配策略）还可能推广到其他涉及不可补给共享资源逐步消耗的在线学习问题中。

Abstract: The one-warehouse multi-store (OWMS) system is a fundamental inventory network in which a nonreplenishable warehouse allocates shared stock across multiple stores over time. Existing OWMS learning policies are built around a fixed target calibrated to the initial average resource rate, but such a fixed-target architecture cannot re-center after realized sales change the remaining resource available per future period. We develop Resource-Adaptive Primal-Dual Learning, a new learning framework that tracks the primal-dual resolving path with censored demand as the remaining-resource state evolves. In each period, the current resource rate indexes the target store allocations and dual variable, while censored sales provide gradient estimates for updating both. The analysis combines expected-sales geometry with a moving-target argument to yield logarithmic expected regret, improving on the state-of-the-art square-root-order guarantees of existing OWMS learning policies. The underlying design and analytical ideas may inform other online learning problems with depleting shared resources. Numerical experiments further demonstrate good finite-horizon performance of a practical variant across different horizon lengths and inventory regimes.

</details>


### [252] [Sequence prediction under a lying oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14102)
*Puspabeethi Samanta,Nikhil Karamchandani,Jayakrishnan Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究m元序列的序贯预测问题，其中学习者在不知道环境真实结果的情况下选择概率分布并承担与预测复杂度相关的代价（该代价源于通过比较查询向说谎oracle进行预测的场景）。作者针对随机和对抗两种环境分别提出算法，并建立了遗憾值的对数上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的序贯预测通常假设代价函数具有特定结构（如对数损失或0-1损失），而本文考虑一种新颖的代价函数，其源于通过比较查询（comparative queries）向说谎oracle（lying oracle）预测的场景，该代价捕获了在这种非标准预测机制下的预测复杂度。需要针对随机与对抗两种环境设计算法并分析遗憾界。

Method: 针对随机环境，作者提出了相应的在线学习算法；针对对抗环境，提出了另一套算法。两类算法均能在不知道环境真实结果的情况下，仅基于部分可观测信息（通过代价反馈）进行学习，并建立了对数形式的遗憾上界。

Result: 在随机和对抗两种环境下，所提出的算法均实现了对数级别的遗憾上界（logarithmic upper bounds on their regret），表明算法的累积损失与最优策略之间的差距随轮次呈对数增长。

Conclusion: 本文为具有特殊代价结构（源自比较查询与说谎oracle场景）的m元序贯预测问题提供了理论保障，证明了在该代价函数下，无论环境是随机还是对抗的，都可以实现遗憾值为对数级别的有效学习。

Abstract: We consider the problem of sequential prediction of an $m$-ary sequence, where at each epoch, (i) the environment selects an outcome from an $m$-ary alphabet, (ii) the learner selects a probability distribution over the same alphabet (unaware of the outcome generated by the environment), and finally, (iii) the learner incurs a cost that depends on the probability assigned to the outcome. The cost function we consider captures the complexity of predicting the outcome generated by the environment, in a scenario where the aforementioned prediction is performed via comparative queries to a lying oracle. We consider both stochastic and adversarial environments, propose algorithms for both settings, and establish logarithmic upper bounds on their regret.

</details>


### [253] [Forecast Collapse in Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14106)
*Shu Wan,Miles Ma,Hank Zhu,Guangqi Liu,Stephen Wang,Qingsong Wen,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在预测1000只美股的小时收益率时会出现"预测坍缩"现象——预测近乎平坦且排序能力差（横截面相关性低），但在预测交易量时该现象几乎消失。作者将原因归结为两点：目标可预测性低限制了校准预测的幅度，以及逐序列目标函数未能捕获横截面结构。为此提出CalibRank目标函数，在Finance1K上使横截面相关性提升近三倍，同时保持预测幅度接近真实目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逐序列评估指标（如MSE）可能掩盖跨序列结构上的失败，而后者正是下游决策（如股票排序、资产配置）所需的。作者旨在揭示这一盲区，并解决校准（calibration）与排序（ranking）之间的权衡问题。

Method: 作者通过跨TSFMs、12个深度学习预测模型和97个公开基准配置进行大规模实证分析，将预测坍缩归因于低可预测性限制校准预测幅度，以及逐序列优化目标无法识别跨序列结构。基于此发现，提出CalibRank目标函数，在平方误差校准与横截面相关性排序之间显式平衡，联合优化两个目标。

Result: 在Finance1K数据集上，CalibRank使横截面相关性提升近三倍（约3倍），同时保持预测幅度接近目标值；在所有测试模型上均改善了相关性。相比之下，直接优化横截面相关性虽然提升排序，但会使预测幅度膨胀一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了传统时间序列评估的盲区：逐序列指标会掩盖下游决策所需的跨序列结构失败。校准-排序权衡是预测坍缩的本质，CalibRank提供了可行的缓解方案，同时警示研究者应关注跨序列评估维度。

Abstract: When forecasting hourly returns for 1,000 US equities, we observe an unexpected phenomenon: predictions become nearly flat and show poor stock ranking, as measured by cross-sectional correlation. We call this forecast collapse. Surprisingly, the phenomenon largely disappears when forecasting trading volume under the same setting. We investigate forecast collapse across time-series foundation models (TSFMs), twelve deep-learning forecasting models, and 97 public benchmark configurations, and find that it is closely tied to target predictability. We identify two distinct reasons behind it: low predictability limits the amplitude of calibrated point forecasts, while per-series objectives leave cross-series structure unidentified. These findings reveal a calibration-ranking tradeoff: optimizing squared error leads to flat predictions, whereas directly optimizing cross-sectional correlation improves ranking but can inflate forecast amplitude by more than an order of magnitude. To address this tradeoff, we introduce CalibRank, a simple objective that balances calibration and ranking. On Finance1K, CalibRank nearly triples cross-sectional correlation while keeping amplitude close to the target, and improves correlation on all tested models. Our results reveal a blind spot in conventional time-series evaluation: per-series metrics can hide failures in cross-series structure needed by downstream decisions.

</details>


### [254] [Learning to Run Power Networks: Effective AlphaZero-inspired Topological Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14114)
*Lukas Zetto,Benjamin Schäfer,Qiong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于AlphaZero和蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的模型驱动方法用于电力网格的自主拓扑重构，通过系统评估奖励函数、观测密度和搜索引导对智能体存活率的影响，发现优化后的AlphaZero方法达到了98.43%的峰值存活率，显著优于PPO方法。研究还发现无先验策略/价值函数引导的MCTS训练效率更高，简单的二值生存奖励比复杂的多目标函数更有效，最终结论是纯强化学习不足以实现可靠的拓扑控制，需要"极简主义"地整合领域启发式、二值奖励和受限的线路负载观测空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着波动性可再生能源在电网中的渗透率不断增加，现代电网承受更大压力。相比传统的再调度措施，通过拓扑动作管理电网拥塞成本更低，但面临庞大的组合动作空间和严格运行约束的挑战。本文旨在探索AlphaZero驱动的MCTS方法能否有效实现电网的主动管理。

Method: 采用AlphaZero风格的方法，结合MCTS进行前瞻性电网管理搜索。系统性地评估了三个关键因素：奖励函数设计（对比二值生存奖励与多目标复杂函数）、观测密度（观测空间的信息丰富程度）、以及搜索引导（是否依赖先验策略网络或价值函数进行MCTS引导），并以PPO作为基线进行性能对比。

Result: 优化后的AlphaZero方法达到98.43%的峰值存活率，显著优于PPO变体。研究还发现：无先验策略/价值函数引导的MCTS训练效率更高；简单的二值生存奖励比复杂的多目标函数提供更有效的搜索引导。

Conclusion: AlphaZero是拓扑控制的强大框架，但纯RL方法不足以实现稳定可靠的电网管理。有效系统需要"极简主义"地整合领域特定的启发式规则、二值奖励以及受限的线路负载观测空间。

Abstract: As the integration of volatile renewable energy sources increases the strain on modern power grids, the use of Reinforcement Learning (RL) for autonomous topological reconfiguration has emerged as a promising research field to keep strained grids stable and operational. Compared to traditional redispatching measures, topological actions offer a cheaper and more cost-effective way to manage grid congestion. However, their implementation is hindered by a vast combinatorial action space and strict operational constraints. This paper investigates the effectiveness of model-based AlphaZero-inspired approaches that utilize Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) for proactive grid management. We systematically evaluate how reward functions, observation density, and search guidance influence an agent's survivability. Our results demonstrate that the optimized AlphaZero approach achieves a peak survivability of 98.43%, significantly outperforming the proximal policy optimization (PPO) variant. We find that conducting the MCTS without guidance from a prior learned policy or value function can enhance training efficiency, and that a straightforward binary survival reward provides more effective search guidance than complex, multi-objective functions. Our findings demonstrate that while AlphaZero is a powerful framework for topological control, pure reinforcement learning is not sufficient; rather, an effective and reliable system requires a 'minimalist' integration of domain-specific heuristics, binary rewards, and a restricted observation space of line loads.

</details>


### [255] [From Fixed Grids to Moving Particles:A Transferable Latent Operator for Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14120)
*Meng Li,Chuqi Chen,Zhengqing Gao,Xi Zhou,Xiao Sun,Yang Xiang,Huaxi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可迁移潜算子（TLO）框架，仅用欧拉观测训练即可零样本迁移到拉格朗日粒子滚动预测，无需拉格朗日监督或任务特定适配。TLO学习欧拉场预测与拉格朗日粒子滚动共享的统一流表示，通过在固定空间坐标或粒子位置查询演化中的潜表示来分别生成欧拉场和拉格朗日轨迹。在五个流体动力学基准上，TLO在欧拉场预测和零样本拉格朗日滚动任务上均优于现有神经算子，有限拉格朗日微调可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉格朗日建模对流体动力学至关重要，但拉格朗日轨迹数据通常比欧拉场数据更难获取，而现有神经算子主要在欧拉表示下训练和评估。这种数据可用性与学习范式之间的错配催生了一个新问题：能否仅基于欧拉观测训练模型，实现从欧拉场预测到拉格朗日粒子滚动的零样本泛化？

Method: 提出Transferable Latent Operator（TLO）框架，学习一个由欧拉场预测和拉格朗日粒子滚动共享的统一流表示。TLO将潜流演化与坐标相关解码解耦：在固定空间坐标查询演化中的潜表示可得到欧拉场；在粒子位置查询速度并递归更新位置则可实现拉格朗日滚动。

Result: 在五个流体动力学基准上，TLO在欧拉场预测和零样本拉格朗日滚动任务上持续优于现有神经算子，且通过有限的拉格朗日微调可获得进一步性能提升。

Conclusion: TLO证明了仅靠欧拉监督训练的模型能够有效泛化到拉格朗日粒子滚动任务，弥合了欧拉与拉格朗日表示之间的学习鸿沟，且少量拉格朗日数据微调可带来额外收益。

Abstract: Lagrangian modeling is vital to fluid dynamics, as it characterizes particle transport and complements the Eulerian representation. However, Lagrangian trajectories are less commonly available than Eulerian fields, while most neural operators are trained and evaluated primarily in the Eulerian representation. This mismatch motivates a new learning problem: can a model trained solely on Eulerian observations generalize zero-shot from Eulerian field prediction to Lagrangian particle rollout, without Lagrangian supervision or task-specific adaptation? To address this problem, we propose the Transferable Latent Operator (TLO), which learns a unified flow representation shared by Eulerian field prediction and Lagrangian particle rollout. TLO decouples latent flow evolution from coordinate-dependent decoding: querying the evolving latent representation at fixed spatial coordinates yields Eulerian fields, whereas querying velocities at particle positions and recursively updating these positions enables Lagrangian rollout. Across five fluid-dynamics benchmarks, TLO consistently outperforms existing neural operators in both Eulerian field prediction and zero-shot Lagrangian rollout, with further gains from limited Lagrangian fine-tuning.

</details>


### [256] [Overcoming Shortcut Learning in Graph Neural Networks through Active Explanation Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14121)
*Taraneh Younesian,Steve Azzolin,Antonio Longa,Francesco Ferrini,Vincenzo Marco De Luca,Stefano Teso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XIGL，一种与架构无关的人类参与式（human-in-the-loop）策略，通过检测GNN解释中的捷径（shortcuts）并结合专家纠正性反馈来去混淆模型，提升其在分布外任务中的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在预测任务中常常无意间利用捷径（即与预测相关但非因果的边、节点和特征），这严重损害了模型在分布外（out-of-distribution）任务中的可靠性。现有方法缺乏有效的机制来识别并移除这类捷径。

Method: XIGL采用两步关键洞察：(1) 通过检查GNN解释来检测模型对捷径的依赖；(2) 专家在识别捷径后提供定制化的纠正反馈以去混淆模型。此外，XIGL支持任意查询策略，并提出一种主动学习策略，优先选择更可能展示捷径行为的解释，从而降低标注和认知成本。

Result: XIGL在多种GNN架构上验证了有效性，涵盖已有的和提出的基于解释的查询策略。其实现代码已开源。

Conclusion: XIGL提供了一种通用且高效的方案，通过人类专家介入和主动学习相结合的方式，系统地消除GNN中的捷径偏差，显著提升模型在分布外场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) can solve prediction tasks by unintentionally exploiting shortcuts---that is, edges, nodes, and features that correlate with but are not causal for the prediction---which compromise their reliability in out-of-distribution tasks. We introduce XIGL, an architecture-agnostic human-in-the-loop strategy for removing such shortcuts from GNNs. Our key insight is twofold. On the one hand, reliance on shortcuts can be detected by inspecting GNN explanations. On the other hand, once made aware of such shortcuts, sufficiently expert users can provide tailored corrective feedback, which helps deconfound the model. XIGL supports any query strategy; however, since corrective feedback can be expensive to acquire, we develop an active learning strategy for prioritizing explanations that are more likely to display shortcut behavior, lowering annotation and cognitive costs. We showcase the effectiveness of XIGL, including both existing and proposed explanation-based strategies, on several GNN architectures. Our implementation is available online.

</details>


### [257] [Smart routes: a system for development and comparison of algorithms for solving vehicle routing problems with realistic constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14140)
*Andrew Soroka,German Mikhelson,Alexander Mescheryakov,Sergey Gerasimov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带时间窗的容量约束车辆路径问题（CVRPTW），比较了精确求解器SCIP、经典启发式算法（LKH、2-OPT、3-OPT、ORTools）和深度学习模型JAMPR的性能，发现对于规模为50的问题，深度学习和经典启发式解已接近SCIP精确解且耗时更少；对于规模为100的问题，SCIP比神经/经典启发式慢约13倍，而在相同时间内找到首个可行解的质量比后者差约50%。作者还开发了Smart Routes平台以集成各类求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着全球城市人口增长，带现实约束的路径优化问题变得极为重要。虽然理论上存在能提供精确最优解的方法，但其指数级复杂度使应用面临挑战，因此需要比较精确求解器与启发式及深度学习方法在CVRPTW问题上的实际性能表现。

Method: 作者针对CVRPTW问题，系统比较了精确求解器SCIP与经典启发式算法（LKH、2-OPT、3-OPT、ORTools框架）以及深度学习模型JAMPR的求解性能，并在问题规模为50和100两种情况下进行实验，同时开发了名为Smart Routes的平台以支持精确、启发式和深度学习模型的集成与自定义算法/数据集的接入。

Result: 问题规模为50时，深度学习与经典启发式解已接近SCIP精确解但所需时间更少；规模为100时，SCIP精确方法比神经及经典启发式慢约13倍且路径成本相当，而在相同时间内SCIP找到的首个可行解质量比启发式差约50%。

Conclusion: 对于中等规模的CVRPTW问题，启发式算法和深度学习模型在保持接近精确解质量的同时，大幅降低了计算时间，成为精确求解器在实际应用中的有效替代方案。

Abstract: The problem of route optimization with realistic constraints is becoming extremely relevant in the face of global urban population growth. While we are aware of approaches that theoretically provide an exact optimal solution, their application becomes challenging as the problem size increases because of exponential complexity. We investigate the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW) and compare solutions obtaining by exact solver SCIP with heuristic algorithms such as LKH, 2-OPT, 3-OPT, the ORTools framework, and the deep learning model JAMPR. We demonstrate that for problem of size 50 deep learning and classical heuristic solutions became close to SCIP exact solution but requires less time. Additionally for problems with size 100, SCIP exact methods around 13 times slower that neural and classical heuristics with the same route cost and on around 50% worse for the first feasible solution on the same time. To conduct experiments, we developed the Smart Routes platform for solving route optimization problems, which includes exact, heuristic, and deep learning models, and facilitates convenient integration of custom algorithms and datasets.

</details>


### [258] [Deep Reinforcement Learning solution for pickup and delivery routing problems with time window and capacity constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14156)
*Andrew Soroka,Alex Meshcheryakov,Sergey Gerasimov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将深度强化学习（改进的JAMPR模型）应用于带容量和时间窗口约束的取送货问题（CPDPTW）。该模型能快速求解中小规模问题的最优解，对200以上规模的大型问题快速给出次优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着全球城市人口增长，车辆取送货路径优化成为一个重要任务。小规模问题可用经典方法解决，但在现实约束（容量和时间窗）下，针对中大规模问题的快速（实时）路由优化仍极具挑战性。

Method: 采用深度强化学习的方法，具体是对JAMPR模型进行改进（modified JAMPR model），将其应用于带容量和时间窗口约束的取送货问题（CPDPTW）。

Result: 所得模型对中小规模问题能快速给出最优解，对超过200个节点的大规模问题能快速给出次优解。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习方法在该类组合优化问题上具有可行性和实用性，兼顾了解质量与求解速度。

Abstract: The task of constructing vehicles optimal routes for pickup and delivery of goods is one of most promising tasks in the context of global urban population growth. Although this kind of problems with small size can be solved by various classical approaches, a fast (or realtime) route optimizer under the constraints of the real world (such as capacity and time windows constraints) for medium-large size problems still remains a highly challenging task. In this work we, for the first time, successfully applied a deep Reinforcing Learning approach (modified JAMPR model) to solve Pickup and Delivery problem with Capacity and Time Window constraints (CPDPTW). We obtained a robust model that gives a fast optimal solution for problems of small and medium size, and gives fast suboptimal solution for problems of larger (> 200) size.

</details>


### [259] [Structure-Guided Spatiotemporal Attention Graph Neural Network for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14177)
*Xuanmian He,Can Li,Wanjing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构引导的时空注意力图神经网络（SGSAN），通过显式学习静态有向依赖图（DDG）作为结构先验，并引入基于InfoNCE的软耦合机制将动态时空注意力锚定到该结构上，在保持顶尖预测精度的同时提供内建可解释性，解决深度时空模型在黑箱交通流预测中缺乏透明度的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度时空模型（图卷积+注意力）虽在交通流预测中表现优异，但缺乏透明度，难以部署于安全关键的城市场景；现有事后（post-hoc）诊断方法易受虚假相关干扰，无法揭示模型内在决策机制，导致可解释性不足和运营信任度受限。

Method: 1）显式学习静态有向依赖图（DDG）以识别交通状态的宏观不变传播路径，替代传统无约束自适应图；2）引入基于InfoNCE的软耦合机制，将动态时空注意力锚定到结构先验，提供机制性解释并防止过度依赖瞬态局部噪声；3）提出解耦两阶段优化框架，解决结构发现与预测误差最小化之间的根本冲突。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，SGSAN取得了最先进的预测精度，同时提供与交通网络物理逻辑有机对齐的内建可解释性。

Conclusion: SGSAN成功兼顾了预测性能与可解释性，通过结构引导的注意力机制使模型决策过程可被理解，为安全关键的智能交通系统提供了更值得信赖的部署方案。

Abstract: Deep spatiotemporal models integrating graph convolutions and attention mechanisms have demonstrated excellent performance in network-level traffic flow prediction, owing to their exceptional ability to capture complex spatiotemporal dependencies. Despite their predictive success, deployment of such models in safety-critical urban systems remains constrained by their inherent lack of transparency. Existing post-hoc diagnostic methods often struggle with spurious correlations and fail to unveil the intrinsic decision-making mechanisms governing traffic dynamics, resulting in suboptimal interpretability and limited operational trustworthiness. To address these challenges, this paper proposes the Structure-Guided Spatiotemporal Attention Graph Neural Network (SGSAN). Departing from traditional architectures that rely on unconstrained adaptive graphs, SGSAN explicitly learns a static Directed Dependency Graph (DDG) to identify the invariant macroscopic propagation paths of traffic states. We further introduce an InfoNCE-based soft-coupling mechanism that anchors the model's dynamic spatiotemporal attention to this structural prior, offering a mechanistic account of the model's decision-making process while ensuring robust forecasting by aligning attention-based reasoning with identified macroscopic dependencies and preventing over-reliance on ephemeral local noise. Furthermore, a decoupled two-stage optimization framework is developed to resolve the fundamental conflict between structural discovery and predictive error minimization. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that SGSAN achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while providing built-in interpretability that organically aligns with the physical logic of traffic networks.

</details>


### [260] [Revisiting Energy-based Tabular Anomaly Detection: Energy and Reconstruction are Complementary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14186)
*Junichiro Niimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将经典深度玻尔兹曼机（DBM）用于表格异常检测，通过均值场能量分数与自编码器重构分数进行秩融合，在两个表格基准（UCI Bank Marketing和NSL-KDD）上显著提升了检测性能（AUROC分别+0.014和+0.002），证明了经典能量模型可作为重构式检测器的非冗余互补视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格异常检测方法（如Isolation Forest、OCSVM、LOF、Autoencoder、VAE、COPOD、ECOD、Deep SVDD等）都仅间接逼近内部分布，且显式能量模型在表格异常检测中基本缺席。鉴于深度学习领域EBM的复兴（如Energy-Based Transformers、JEPA），作者欲重访经典DBM，检验其均值场能量与重构式分数的组合是否优于同溯源配对方法。

Method: 使用两隐藏层DBM作为能量模型，其均值场能量作为异常分数；在UCI Bank Marketing和NSL-KDD两个表格基准上，将DBM与八个经典及现代基线（含Autoencoder、VAE、Isolation Forest、OCSVM、LOF、COPOD、ECOD、Deep SVDD等）在二十个随机种子下对比；并通过秩融合将DBM能量分数与Autoencoder重构分数结合评估互补性。

Result: DBM均值场能量在Bank Marketing上持平最强基线（Autoencoder），在NSL-KDD上统计学显著击败之，并在两个数据集上显著超越其余七个基线。通过秩融合，DBM能量与Autoencoder结合在两个数据集上均取得统计学显著提升（Bank Marketing AUROC+0.014, p<0.01；NSL-KDD +0.002, p<0.001）；所有非DBM派生基座模型与AE融合时均未提升或显著降低了整体性能。

Conclusion: 经典EBM（以DBM为代表）应当被纳入表格异常检测工具库，作为对当前主导的重构式分数的非冗余互补视角。实验证据表明DBM能量分数与Autoencoder重构分数的融合能带来一致且统计学显著的性能增益。

Abstract: Tabular anomaly detection is dominated by classical density-proxy methods (Isolation Forest, OCSVM, LOF), reconstruction-based detectors (Autoencoders, VAEs), and modern non-parametric scorers (COPOD, ECOD, Deep SVDD), all of which approximate the inlier distribution only indirectly; explicit energy-based models are largely absent. Motivated by the recent revival of EBMs in deep learning (e.g., Energy-Based Transformers, JEPA), we revisit the classical Deep Boltzmann Machine (DBM) for this task and hypothesize that its mean-field energy combines more effectively with a reconstruction-based score than same-lineage pairs do. We evaluate a two-hidden-layer DBM on two tabular benchmarks spanning distinct domains (UCI Bank Marketing and NSL-KDD) against eight classical and modern baselines across twenty random seeds. The DBM mean-field energy matches the strongest baseline (the Autoencoder) on Bank Marketing and statistically beats it on NSL-KDD, while significantly outperforming the remaining seven on both datasets. When fused with the Autoencoder via rank fusion, the DBM energy yields a statistically significant improvement on both datasets (AUROC=+0.014, p<0.01 on Bank Marketing; +0.002, p<0.001 on NSL-KDD); every non-DBM-derived base model instead fails to improve or significantly degrades the AE-paired ensemble. Our position is that classical EBMs, exemplified by the DBM, deserve a place in the tabular anomaly detection toolbox as a non-redundant complementary view to the reconstruction-based scores that dominate current practice.

</details>


### [261] [KV Cache Compression Through the Lens of Transform Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14191)
*Hannah Laus,Claudio Mayrink Verdun,Hao Wang,Flavio du Pin Calmon,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Attention-Aware Transform Coding (AATC)，一种基于变换编码和反向注水的KV缓存压缩方法，通过最小化注意力感知的失真（而非仅最小化缓存重建误差）来实现KV缓存的低比特量化。作者证明了在白噪声量化模型下，注意力感知的失真可分解为键和值贡献的加和形式，并在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct上，在约5.8倍压缩率下实现近无损精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存是长上下文推理中的主要内存瓶颈。现有量化方法将KV缓存统一用低精度数据类型表示，但仅关注最小化缓存本身的重建误差，忽略了量化误差通过注意力机制传播的影响，导致精度下降。

Method: 首先证明在白噪声量化模型下，注意力感知的失真可以分解为键和值贡献的加和，且跨token和通道可因子化。基于此理论，利用信号处理中的变换编码和速率-失真理论中的反向注水工具，设计AATC方法，在标定集上分配比特数以最小化注意力感知失真。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct上，跨LongBench、RULER、GSM8K、MMLU-Pro和MATH-500等多个基准评估，AATC在约5.8倍压缩率下实现近无损精度，而各类基线方法在至少某些设置下出现精度退化。

Conclusion: 通过将注意力机制的影响纳入量化失真度量，AATC相比现有方法在相同甚至更高压缩率下保持更高的精度，为KV缓存压缩提供了更高效的理论驱动方案。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache stores information from past tokens and is a major memory bottleneck in long-context inference. Existing quantization methods address this bottleneck by representing the KV cache uniformly with lower-precision data types and designing quantization schemes to minimize reconstruction error in the cache itself, without accounting for how that error propagates through attention mechanisms. We prove that, under a white-noise quantization model, the expected attention-aware distortion decomposes into additive key and value contributions that factor across tokens and channels. Building on transform coding and reverse water-filling, which are classical tools from signal processing and rate-distortion theory, we introduce Attention-Aware Transform Coding (AATC), which allocates bits over a calibration set to minimize attention-aware distortion. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, evaluated across LongBench, RULER, GSM8K, MMLU-Pro, and MATH-500, our method achieves near-lossless accuracy at approximately $5.8\times$ compression, whereas each baseline degrades in at least some settings.

</details>


### [262] [MINT: A Universal Zero-Shot Predictor for Transaction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14198)
*Parameswaran Kamalaruban,Viktor Drobnyi,Maeve Madigan,Julia Rozanova,David Sutton,Stuart Burrell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MINT框架，将预训练的交易序列编码器与仅解码器的LLM通过轻量级嵌入注入、交易-语言对齐和指令微调连接起来，实现交易数据的零样本预测推理。MINT在分布内和分布外预测问答任务上达到SOTA性能，同时显著减少输入tokens、延迟和内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行需要分析顺序交易数据以进行欺诈预防、信用风险评估和个性化推荐。现有的支付基础模型虽能编码交易序列为上下文嵌入，但不支持灵活的光零零样本推理；而基于LLM的零样本方法未能充分利用交易数据中的预测信号，且依赖昂贵的文本序列化或扩展性差的任务特定架构。

Method: MINT框架由三部分组成：1) 轻量级嵌入注入（lightweight embedding injection），将预训练交易序列编码器产生的紧凑嵌入注入到仅解码器LLM中；2) 交易-语言对齐（transaction-language alignment），对齐交易嵌入与语言表示空间；3) 指令微调（instruction tuning），使模型能够理解并回答预测性问答任务。

Result: MINT在分布内和分布外预测问答问题上均达到SOTA性能，相比文本序列化基线大幅减少了输入tokens、延迟和内存消耗。通过对表示、对齐策略、训练数据和时间长度的综合分析，验证了紧凑交易嵌入相比文本序列化在多模态推理和零样本预测任务中是更优的交易表示方法。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了紧凑的交易嵌入相较于文本序列化在多模态推理和零样本预测任务中的优越性，为支付基础模型与LLM的融合提供了一种高效、可扩展且性能更优的范式。

Abstract: Banks analyse sequential financial transaction data to perform many tasks, including fraud prevention, credit risk assessment and offer personalization. To improve the predictive accuracy of these tasks, Payments Foundation Models encode transaction sequence data as rich contextual embeddings, which can then be provided to task-specific models as features. However, these Foundation Models are not designed for flexible zero-shot reasoning across novel downstream prediction tasks, limiting their adaptability and utility. Existing LLM-based approaches to zero-shot prediction often fail to fully exploit the predictive signal within transaction data, while relying on costly text serialization or task-specific architectures that scale poorly. To address these limitations, we present the Multimodal Instruction Network for Transactions (MINT), a framework that connects a pretrained transaction sequence encoder to a decoder-only LLM through lightweight embedding injection, transaction-language alignment, and instruction tuning. We find that MINT achieves state-of-the-art predictive question-answering performance in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution questions, while substantially reducing input tokens, latency, and memory consumption compared to text-serialization baselines. Through comprehensive analyses of representations, alignment strategies, training data, and history length, we establish that compact transaction embeddings are a superior approach to transaction representation than text serialization for multimodal reasoning and zero-shot prediction tasks.

</details>


### [263] [Adaptive Protection for Evolutionary Feature Construction in Symbolic Regression with Application to Credit Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14209)
*Hengzhe Zhang,Qi Chen,Bing Xue,Lean Yu,Wolfgang Banzhaf,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应保护机制，利用特征重要性度量在进化过程中选择性保留已构造的重要特征，在98个回归基准数据集和2个信用分类数据集上验证了该方法能一致地提升解质量，并超越了符号回归的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化特征构造在符号回归中表现出色，但现有方法缺乏显式机制来保留进化过程中发现的重要构造特征，遗传算子会破坏有效特征导致宝贵遗传物质的丢失。

Method: 引入基于特征重要性度量的自适应保护机制，对更重要的构造特征提供更强的保护，同时允许次重要特征被修改并整合来自重要特征的有用构建块。该方法在多种特征重要性计算方法下评估，并测试了不同基学习器的鲁棒性。

Result: 在98个回归基准数据集上，所提机制一致地提升了基线方法的解质量；在2个信用分类数据集上的实验表明该方法能有效扩展到符号回归之外的搜索任务。

Conclusion: 基于特征重要性的自适应保护机制能够有效保留进化中发现的重要构造特征，提升进化特征构造的性能，且具有跨任务类型和跨基学习器的泛化能力。

Abstract: Evolutionary feature construction has shown strong promise in symbolic regression by automatically discovering informative transformations of input features that enhance a simple base learner. However, existing approaches often lack explicit mechanisms to preserve important constructed features discovered during evolution, and valuable genetic material can be lost when genetic operators disrupt effective features. This paper introduces an adaptive protection mechanism that leverages feature importance metrics to selectively preserve constructed features during evolution. The mechanism provides stronger protection for more important constructed features while still allowing less important features to be modified and to incorporate useful building blocks from more important features. We evaluate the approach using multiple feature importance calculation methods and demonstrate its robustness across different base learners. Experimental results on 98 regression benchmark datasets show that the proposed mechanism consistently improves solution quality over baseline approaches, and experiments on two credit classification datasets demonstrate that the method also extends effectively to improve search effectiveness beyond symbolic regression.

</details>


### [264] [Training Fair Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14211)
*Patrik Kenfack,Jesse C. Cresswell,Anthony L. Caterini,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Ulrich Aïvodji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairTFM，一种将公平性约束直接整合进表格基础模型（TFM）训练中的方法，通过在合成公平性任务上训练并采用带梯度反转层的公平性感知架构，使模型能够在前向传播中直接实现公平预测，在132个公平性任务上验证了在保持竞争力的准确率的同时显著提升公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFMs）已成为表格预测任务的主流方法，广泛应用于高风险的决策场景，但其公平性属性尚未得到充分探索。现有公平性技术无法直接应用于上下文学习（in-context learning）范式，且训练数据中敏感属性的可用性有限，这两大挑战阻碍了TFMs的公平性保障。

Method: 提出FairTFM训练策略：1）通过合成公平性任务来扩展训练数据，解决敏感属性访问受限的问题；2）采用公平性感知架构，利用梯度反转层（Gradient Reversal Layer）鼓励模型学习对敏感属性不变的表示；3）将公平性约束直接嵌入TFM训练过程中，使模型在单个前向传播中即可实现公平预测。

Result: 在132个公平性任务上的实验表明，FairTFM在保持竞争力准确率的同时，能够持续显著改善公平性指标。

Conclusion: FairTFM有效解决了TFM中公平性整合的核心挑战，通过合成数据和公平性感知架构，成功实现了无需任务特定训练即可在上下文学习框架内提供公平预测的能力，为表格基础模型在高风险决策场景中的公平应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as leading methods for tabular predictive tasks, leveraging in-context learning to predict on new data without task-specific training. Despite the increased use of TFMs in high-stakes decision-making, their fairness properties remain largely unexplored. In this work, we incorporate fairness constraints directly into TFM training, enabling fair predictions in a single forward pass. Our approach addresses two key challenges: limited access to sensitive attributes in training data, and the incompatibility of existing fairness techniques with the in-context learning paradigm. We propose FairTFM, a scalable training strategy based on synthetic fairness tasks and a fairness-aware architecture using a gradient reversal layer, which encourages the model to learn representations invariant to sensitive attributes. Experiments on 132 fairness tasks show consistent improvements in fairness while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [265] [Connected Subspace Clustering: Hardness, a Scalable Heuristic, and an Application to Sea Level Geodesy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14215)
*Johanna Hillebrand,Jan Höckendorff,Jürgen Kusche,Kelin Luo,Heiko Röglin,Melanie Schmidt,Christian Sohler,Bernd Uebbing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连通子空间聚类问题：给定高维点和连通图，将数据划分为k个连通簇，最小化各簇到其最优m'维仿射子空间的平方距离之和。作者证明了即使m'=0且图含洞，该问题也难以近似(NP-hard, 近似比下界Ω(n^{1/2-ε}))，并提出一种Lloyd式启发式算法，通过交替执行子空间拟合和迭代合并过程来强制连通性。该算法在大地测量学应用中表现优异，在160种全球海平面时间序列配置中，73.75%的情况下优于其他修复策略，且产生的区域能隔离与气候指数(如ENSO、IOD)一致的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典聚类方法未考虑物理位置的连通性约束，而许多科学与工程应用中(如大地测量学中需识别海洋表面的连通区域进行主成分分析)，聚类的内部相似性与物理连续性同等重要。现有无约束方法会留下大量不连通的碎片，无法满足实际需求。

Method: 提出连通子空间聚类问题的形式化定义，给出该问题的NP-hard性证明(即使是最简单情形)。设计Lloyd式启发式算法：交替进行基于子空间的簇分配(与k均值/子空间聚类类似)和基于迭代合并的连通性修复过程，确保最终返回恰好k个连通区域。

Result: 无约束方法最多留下1,966个不连通碎片且代价更高；所提算法在160种全球海平面时间序列配置的实验中，合并式修复策略在73.75%的情况下优于四种策略中的其他方法，并在所有测试簇数下一致优于(Ward)方法等竞争对手。

Conclusion: 尽管问题在理论上被证明是NP-hard且不可近似，所提出的启发式在实际应用中有效。生成的连通区域能够隔离与ENSO和IOD等气候指数一致的信号。该方法虽源于大地测量学，但同样适用于气候场、遥感、神经影像和传感器网络等空间嵌入的多元时间序列。

Abstract: Constrained optimization extends classical optimization by integrating side information, making it widely applicable across scientific and engineering domains. Consider a setting where we measure variables at different physical locations. When grouping these measurements, we often want clusters that are both internally similar and physically coherent. Thus, we have a constrained clustering problem where the constraint models coherence. Motivated by an application in geodesy, where contiguous regions of the sea surface must be identified for principal component analysis, we introduce the Connected Subspace Clustering problem: given high-dimensional points and a connectivity graph, partition them into $k$ connected clusters, minimizing their total squared distance to the clusters' best-fit $m'$-dimensional affine subspaces. We prove that, even for $m' = 0$ and a grid graph with holes, the problem is NP-hard to approximate within $Ω(n^{1/2-\varepsilon})$ for every $\varepsilon>0$, where $n$ is the number of measurements. We then introduce an efficient Lloyd-style heuristic that alternates subspace fitting with an iterative merging procedure to enforce connectivity. Our method returns exactly $k$ connected regions by construction, whereas unconstrained methods leave up to $1{,}966$ disconnected fragments at higher cost. In a study of 160 configurations on global sea level time series, our merging-based repair is the strongest of four strategies in $73.75\%$ of cases, and consistently outperforms competitors such as (connected) Ward's method across all tested cluster counts. The resulting regions isolate signals aligning with climate indices such as the El Nino-Southern Oscillation and Indian Ocean Dipole. Although developed for geodesy, the approach applies to other spatially embedded multivariate time series, such as climate fields, remote sensing, neuroimaging, and sensor networks.

</details>


### [266] [AutoSchema: Live Schema Grounding for Agentic Text-to-Sparql over Heterogeneous Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14228)
*Yiming Zhang,Koji Tsuda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoSchema，一个无需训练的实时模式接地（live schema grounding）框架，使语言模型代理能够直接从当前SPARQL端点获取模式证据来查询生命科学知识图谱，无需预先创建和维护人工策展的元数据文件。在多个生物医学和化学KGQA基准上，AutoSchema相比TogoMCP提升了事实性问答准确率并减少了工具调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命科学知识图谱通过SPARQL提供了大量结构化数据，但每个资源使用不同的模式（schema）、标识符和链接。现有方案如TogoMCP依赖人工策展的Metadata Interoperability Exchange文件，创建和维护这些文件仍需语言模型辅助起草、验证和人工审查，成本高昂且难以扩展。因此需要一种能够直接从当前端点获取模式证据的通用方法。

Method: AutoSchema是一个无需训练的实时模式接地通用框架，包含四个关键步骤：（1）检查实时模式（inspecting live schemas）；（2）将问题中的实体名称映射到图标识符；（3）探索关系路径；（4）在迭代查询构建过程中发现资源之间的可能连接。

Result: 在Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA、Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA、Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA（BioASQ Task B）和Chemistry Knowledge Graph Transfer四个任务上的实验表明：AutoSchema在生物医学KGQA任务中的平均事实性准确率优于TogoMCP，在纵向BioASQ评估中也有稳定提升；同时减少了迭代预算耗尽的情况，并在核心评估中平均使用更少的工具调用。迁移实验初步证明实时模式接地可以支持不规则和此前未见过的图，而无需预先创建策展模式文件。

Conclusion: 实时模式接地作为一种无需训练、无需预先策展模式文件的通用方法，能够有效支持语言模型代理查询异构的生命科学知识图谱资源，提高问答准确率并降低工具调用开销，为知识图谱查询自动化提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Life science knowledge graphs make large collections of structured data available through SPARQL, but each resource uses its own schema, identifiers, and links. TogoMCP helps language model agents query these resources by providing curated Metadata Interoperability Exchange files. Creating and maintaining these files still requires language model assisted drafting, validation, and manual review. We study \emph{live schema grounding}, where an agent obtains the schema evidence needed for a question directly from the current endpoints. We present \textsc{autoschema}, a general framework for live schema grounding that requires no training. It inspects live schemas, maps entity names in a question to graph identifiers, explores relation paths, and finds possible connections between resources during iterative query construction. We use TogoMCP as our main comparison framework. We evaluate \textsc{autoschema} on Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA, Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA, Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA over BioASQ Task B, and Chemistry Knowledge Graph Transfer to a previously undocumented RDF graph. \textsc{autoschema} improves mean factoid accuracy over TogoMCP in the biomedical KGQA tasks and gives consistent gains in the longitudinal BioASQ evaluation. It also reduces iteration budget exhaustion and uses fewer tool calls on average in the core evaluation. The transfer study gives preliminary evidence that live schema grounding can support irregular and previously unseen graphs without first creating a curated schema file.

</details>


### [267] [Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14264)
*Utkarsh Agarwal,Vamshi Bonagiri,Raul Astudillo,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MOBO-Merge框架，将模型合并中的参数选择建模为黑盒多目标优化问题，使用多目标贝叶斯优化在有限评估预算下近似Pareto前沿，实验表明其在多数场景下优于随机搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并通过在权重空间直接组合训练好的模型，是一种计算高效替代微调的方法。然而合参数的选取面临三个困难：下游评估成本高、梯度不可用、来源模型的能力可能相互冲突。因此需要一种高效的自动参数选择方法。

Method: 将合并参数选择形式化为黑盒多目标优化（MOBO）问题，提出MOBO-Merge框架——一个与合并算子无关的方法，利用多目标贝叶斯优化在有限评估预算下近似Pareto前沿。在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B上，采用Linear、SLERP、TIES和逐块（block-wise）合并算子进行双模型（指令-数学）和三模型（指令-数学-代码）设置下的评估。

Result: 在12组比较中，MOBO-Merge在11组中获得了比随机搜索更高的平均超体积（hypervolume）。在一维Linear插值上增益较小，但在TIES、逐块以及三目标搜索中增益显著。没有统一的优胜合并算子：TIES在四个家族-设置组合中的三个表现最佳，而Block-Linear 4x在Llama三模型合并中最强。

Conclusion: 多目标贝叶斯优化作为表达性合并参数化的搜索层是有价值的，MOBO-Merge为模型合并中的参数选择提供了有效的自动化方案。

Abstract: Model merging combines trained models directly in weight space, offering a compute-efficient alternative to additional fine-tuning. Selecting merge parameters is nevertheless difficult because downstream evaluations are expensive, gradients are unavailable, and source capabilities can conflict. We formulate merge-parameter selection as a black-box multi-objective optimization problem and introduce MOBO-Merge, a merge-operator agnostic framework that uses multi-objective Bayesian optimization to approximate the Pareto front under a limited evaluation budget. We evaluate Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B in two-model instruction-math and three-model instruction-math-code settings using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and block-wise merge operators. On held-out benchmark partitions, MOBO-Merge obtains higher mean hypervolume than random search in 11 of 12 reported comparisons. The gain is small for one-dimensional Linear interpolation but substantially larger for several TIES, block-wise, and three-objective searches. No merge operator is uniformly best: TIES leads in three of four family-setting combinations, whereas Block-Linear 4x is strongest for the Llama three-model merge. These results show that multi-objective Bayesian optimization is valuable as a search layer for expressive merge parameterizations.

</details>


### [268] [Convex losses and their applications to SVM, SVR, and Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14288)
*Filippo Portera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多种用于二分类SVM和神经网络的新型凸损失函数，并通过粒子群优化算法在原始SVM和神经网络中应用这些损失。实验表明，尽管理论上将模式相关性纳入损失函数可能提升泛化性能，但实际上嵌套交叉验证结果与标准损失无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以将模式相关性（pattern correlations）纳入损失函数以增强分类模型的泛化性能，为此提出多种凸损失函数作为标准损失的推广。

Method: 提出了多种新型凸损失函数（为标准损失的泛化），用于SVM原始形式与神经网络的二分类任务。SVM原始问题通过粒子群优化（PSO）算法求解，并使用嵌套交叉验证（Nested Cross-Validation）评估各损失函数的泛化性能。

Result: 在多个小数据集上的初步实验表明，使用新损失函数与使用标准损失的泛化度量结果相同，即新损失并未显著提升泛化性能，尽管理论上存在增强的可能性。

Conclusion: 将模式相关性纳入损失函数在理论上可能提升泛化性能，但在当前实验设置下并未带来实际改善；该研究为初步探索，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: We propose multiple new convex losses for SVM and Neural Networks, applied to binary classification tasks. While there are practical limitations in exploiting them with the dual SVM models, we are able to use them with SVM primal formulation and Neural Networks. In detail, the primal SVM problem with the modified losses has been solved with the Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm. We prove that the proposed losses are a generalization of the standard loss, and we experiment them with several small data-sets. This preliminary study shows that using pattern correlations
  inside the loss function could in theory enhance the generalization performances on some data-sets. To evaluate the performance of each loss, we adopt a Nested Cross-Validation procedure. Results show that generalization measures are the same with or without the new losses.

</details>


### [269] [Detecting Contaminated Code-Generation Prompt Batches via Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14303)
*Francesco Quinzan,Noor Munir,Yishun Lu,Stephen Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CodeSIFT，一种无需威胁模型预设的检测方法，利用影响函数识别诱导代码大模型生成不安全代码的恶意提示批次。在三个开源代码LLM（3B-7B参数）上，AUROC最高达0.98，显著优于静态分析基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代码生成中容易受到恶意提示的影响而生成不安全实现。现有防御方法依赖预定义的威胁模型或已知漏洞模式，无法有效应对新型攻击。因此需要一种不依赖先验攻击类别知识的通用检测方法。

Method: CodeSIFT采用威胁模型无关的策略：通过影响函数（influence functions）测量生成代码在参数空间中的影响，再利用统计检验判断候选提示集是否偏离良性参考分布。无需识别具体漏洞类型，而是检测异常的模型行为。

Result: 在三个3B至7B参数的开源代码LLM上进行评估，AUROC最高达0.98（在中高注入率下），误报率校准良好，且显著优于静态分析基线。同时构建了两个覆盖多种漏洞类型的基准数据集。

Conclusion: 基于影响函数的检测是识别恶意代码生成提示的有前景方向，无需预先了解底层攻击类别即可实现有效检测。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet they remain vulnerable to prompts that elicit insecure implementations. Existing defenses typically rely on predefined threat models or known vulnerability patterns, limiting their effectiveness against novel attacks. We propose CodeSIFT, a threat-model-agnostic detection method that leverages influence functions to identify batches of prompts that induce anomalous model behavior. Rather than detecting specific vulnerabilities, CodeSIFT measures the parameter-space influence of generated code and uses a statistical test to determine whether a candidate prompt set deviates from a benign reference distribution. To evaluate our approach, we introduce two benchmark datasets covering a variety of vulnerabilities. We evaluate CodeSIFT on three open-weight code LLMs ranging from 3B to 7B parameters, achieving AUROC scores of up to 0.98 at moderate-to-high injection rates, while maintaining well-calibrated false positive rates and substantially outperforming static analysis baselines. These results suggest that influence-function-based detection is a promising direction for identifying malicious code-generation prompts without requiring prior knowledge of the underlying attack class.

</details>


### [270] [Quantum Multi-Armed Bandits and Linear Bandits: Lower Bounds and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14319)
*Maoli Liu,Zhuohua Li,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究量子多臂老虎机（QMAB）和量子线性老虎机（QLB）的后悔界，首次证明QMAB的最小最大下界为Ω(K log(T/K))，有限动作QLB的下界为Ω(d log(T/d))，并给出一个基于设计消除的有限动作QLB算法，其后悔值线性依赖于维度d，优于先前的d²依赖并匹配下界（差对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作给出了QMAB的O(K log T)后悔和d维QLB的O(d² polylog T)后悔，但关键问题悬而未决：K log T的量级是否不可避免？d²的维度依赖能否改进？这些问题的答案决定了Wan等人提出的"与T无关的后悔是否可实现"这一根本问题。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 利用多项式方法和Remez型不等式（适用于三角多项式）证明高置信度单臂量子测试下界，用于区分固定奖励均值与区间内的替代值；(2) 通过bandit-to-testing约化将单臂下界推广为QMAB的后悔下界；(3) 通过线性嵌入获得有限动作QLB的下界；(4) 在算法方面，设计基于设计的消除算法，结合低偏差低方差量子均值估计器与小支撑G-最优设计，并通过与设计权重匹配的查询分配实现维度依赖从d²降到d^(3/2)，再通过方差聚合重构误差消除剩余√d因子。

Result: 首次建立QMAB的Ω(K log(T/K))和有限动作QLB的Ω(d log(T/d))最小最大后悔下界，解决了Wan等人提出的核心问题。同时，当动作集大小为poly(d)时，所提算法的后悔为O(d polylog T)，将先前的d²依赖改进为线性d，与下界匹配至多对数因子。

Conclusion: 该工作从上下界两方面完整刻画了量子多臂老虎机和有限动作量子线性老虎机的后悔复杂性。下界证明表明与T无关的后悔在信息论意义上是不可实现的（对于线性老虎机，后悔仍需随T对数增长），而提出的消除算法在有限动作场景下达到了维度最优（至多差对数因子）。此外，该工作中通过多项式方法和Remez不等式建立的量子测试下界技术可望推广到更一般的量子学习问题。

Abstract: We study quantum multi-armed bandits (QMAB) and quantum linear bandits (QLB) in the model of Wan et al. [2023], where the learner queries each arm or action through a quantum reward oracle or its inverse. Prior work gives algorithms over horizon $T$ with regret $O(K\log T)$ for QMAB with $K$ arms and $O(d^2\operatorname{polylog} T)$ for $d$-dimensional QLB. This leaves open whether the $K\log T$ scale is unavoidable and whether the $d^2$ dependence can be improved. We prove the first minimax lower bounds of $Ω(K\log(T/K))$ for QMAB and $Ω(d\log(T/d))$ for finite-action QLB, resolving the question raised by Wan et al. [2023] of whether regret independent of $T$ is achievable. At the heart of our argument is a high-confidence single-arm quantum testing lower bound for distinguishing a fixed reward mean from an interval of alternatives, proved by the polynomial method and a Remez-type inequality for trigonometric polynomials. A bandit-to-testing reduction then lifts it to the QMAB lower bound, while a linear embedding gives the finite-action QLB lower bound. Complementing the lower bounds, we give a design-based elimination algorithm for finite-action QLB. When the action set has size $\operatorname{poly}(d)$, its regret is linear in $d$, improving the prior $d^2$ dependence and matching our lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors. The algorithm couples a low-bias low-variance quantum mean estimator with a small-support $G$-optimal design through a query allocation matched to the design weights. The design-based elimination reduces the dimension dependence from $d^2$ to $d^{3/2}$ when using Quantum Monte Carlo estimates. The low-variance estimator then makes reconstruction error aggregate through variance rather than worst-case absolute error, removing the remaining $\sqrt d$ factor.

</details>


### [271] [Non-Parametric Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction via State-Conditioned Transition Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14349)
*Michael Fore,Akshay Jain,Justin Downes,Rohan Pradhan,Duncan Botti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的多元轨迹预测方法，用零参数、零GPU的查询表方法达到与5700万参数transformer相当甚至更优的精度，尤其在数据稀缺场景下表现稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的轨迹预测模型（如TrAISformer）需要大量训练数据和GPU资源，且在新地理区域部署时因数据不足而性能急剧退化。作者希望提出一种无需训练的替代方案，能够在数据稀缺场景下保持稳定性能，并去除GPU训练需求。

Method: 构建历史状态到下一位置的转移表，使用包含空间邻近性、方位、速度和时序上下文在内的乘积核进行近邻检索。两种推理模式共享该表示：多样性惩罚采样生成覆盖多条合理路线的轨迹，而束搜索寻找最高似然路径。

Result: 在TrAISformer基准（丹麦海事AIS数据）上，该方法在完整数据下达到与transformer相当/竞争的精度；在数据稀缺场景下大幅优于transformer——在仅10%训练数据时仍保持稳定，而TrAISformer性能急剧退化。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了一个数量级更少的历史数据即可在新地理区域部署，且完全无需GPU训练，为轨迹预测提供了高效实用且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: We present a training-free method for multi-modal trajectory prediction that achieves comparable accuracy to a 57M-parameter transformer while requiring no GPU and zero learned parameters. The method builds a transition table of historical state-to-next-position pairs and retrieves neighbors using a product kernel over spatial proximity, bearing, speed, and temporal context. Two inference modes operate over this shared representation: diversity-penalized sampling produces trajectories covering distinct plausible routes, while beam search finds the highest-likelihood path. On the TrAISformer benchmark (Danish Maritime AIS), our method achieves competitive accuracy at full data availability and dramatically outperforms the transformer in data-scarce regimes---remaining stable down to 10% of training data where TrAISformer degrades catastrophically. This enables deployment in new geographic regions from an order of magnitude less historical data, and with no GPU training.

</details>


### [272] [Mind the Long Tail: Understanding the Difficulty of Delay Detection in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14367)
*Keyvan Amiri Elyasi,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了业务流程中延迟检测（delay detection）的固有难度。通过对14个事件日志的研究发现，剩余时间分布通常严重右偏（右偏），只有少数案例存在大幅延迟；现有模型能较好捕捉分布众数，但在高延迟案例上表现不佳；预测不确定性随延迟幅度增大而增加（异方差性）。基于这些发现评估了缓解不平衡问题的方法，但收益有限，表明核心问题可能并非类别不平衡，而是延迟案例固有的更高不确定性。利用这种相关性可以显著改善延迟案例的识别，不确定性感知建模是未来PPM研究的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测过程监控（PPM）中的剩余时间预测虽已有大量深度学习架构的进展，但延迟检测本身的固有难度缺乏系统研究。既有评估通常使用聚合指标，无法揭示模型在目标分布上的表现差异，尤其是对运营上最关键的、具有大幅延迟的案例。作者希望填补这一空白，深入理解延迟检测困难的根本来源。

Method: 作者在14个事件日志上系统分析了剩余时间的分布特征（偏度）、现有模型在不同延迟区间上的预测表现、预测不确定性与延迟幅度之间的关系（异方差性检验），并评估了多种缓解不平衡问题的策略（如重加权、重采样等），同时探索利用不确定性与延迟相关性来改进延迟案例识别的方法。

Result: 研究发现：剩余时间分布典型地严重右偏，仅小部分案例呈现大幅延迟；现有模型能较好捕捉分布众数但在高延迟案例上表现不佳；预测不确定性随延迟增大而增加。缓解不平衡的措施收益有限，说明问题核心可能不是不平衡本身而是延迟案例的更高不确定性；利用不确定性与延迟的相关性可显著提升延迟案例的识别效果。

Conclusion: 延迟检测的困难并非主要源于样本不平衡，而在于高延迟案例天然具有更高的固有不确定性。不确定性感知建模被识别为未来PPM研究的一个重要方向，通过利用不确定性-延迟相关性可以有效改善关键延迟案例的识别能力。

Abstract: The early detection of delayed cases in business processes is a critical capability for organizations. Predictive process monitoring (PPM) supports this task by using historical event logs to predict the remaining time of ongoing cases, enabling timely interventions to avoid missed deadlines and service level violations. Although remaining time prediction has advanced considerably through sophisticated deep learning architectures, little is known about the intrinsic difficulty of delay detection itself. Since performance is typically assessed using aggregate metrics, prior work provides limited insight into how models perform across the target distribution, especially on the operationally most critical cases with large delays. In this paper, we address this gap by analyzing the difficulty of delay detection. Across 14 event logs, we show that remaining times are typically strongly right-skewed, with only a small fraction of cases exhibiting large delays. Existing models capture the mode of this distribution well but perform poorly on high-delay cases. We further uncover pronounced heteroscedasticity, showing that predictive uncertainty increases with delay magnitude. Based on these findings, we evaluate approaches to mitigate the imbalance problem, but find only limited benefits, suggesting that the key underlying problem may not be imbalance but higher uncertainty associated with delayed cases. We show that this correlation can be exploited to substantially improve the identification of delayed cases. Overall, our work provides new insights into the sources of difficulty in delay detection and identifies uncertainty-aware modeling as a promising direction for future PPM research.

</details>


### [273] [Catching the Imposter: Self-Supervised Learning of Physical Coherence with Cross-Entity Feature Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"imposter"判别式自监督预训练任务，通过替换实体特征子集为其他实体的真实观测值，迫使编码器学习跨特征的物理依赖关系。在ERA5-Land再分析数据（21个环境变量）上评估，并在气候分类、碳通量估算和径流预测等七项下游任务中验证，发现最佳预训练任务取决于下游任务类型，imposter可与现有SSL目标互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据中实体的特征通常由物理定律共同决定，但现有自监督学习目标基本忽略了这种物理一致性。作者希望利用这一物理学约束作为新的自监督信号来源。

Method: 提出imposter预训练任务：将某实体的部分特征替换为另一实体捐赠的真实观测值（每个捐赠值单独来看都是合理的），训练编码器识别被替换的特征。由于无法通过单变量统计破解，该任务迫使模型学习跨特征的物理依赖关系。在统一架构和预训练预算下系统比较多种SSL目标。

Result: 最佳预训练任务依赖于下游任务类型而非单一目标全局最优；imposter在与现有SSL目标结合时提供互补信息。这是首次在共享架构和预训练预算下对陆面建模SSL目标进行系统比较。

Conclusion: 物理一致性是科学基础模型有价值的自监督新来源，imposter任务可作为现有SSL目标的有力补充。

Abstract: Scientific data often describe entities whose features are jointly governed by the laws of physics, yet existing self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives largely ignore this physical coherence. We introduce imposter, a discriminative pretext task that replaces subsets of an entity's features with real observations donated by another entity and trains the encoder to identify the swapped features. Because every donated value is individually plausible, the task can only be solved by learning cross-feature physical dependencies. We evaluate the proposed objectives on global ERA5-Land reanalysis data using 21 environmental variables and assess the learned representations on seven downstream tasks spanning climate classification, carbon flux estimation, and streamflow prediction. Our study includes, to our knowledge, the first systematic comparison of self-supervised objectives for land-surface modeling under a shared architecture and pre-training budget. We find that the most effective pretext task depends on the downstream task family rather than any single objective's superiority, and that imposter provides complementary information when combined with existing SSL objectives. These results suggest that physical coherence is a valuable new source of self-supervision for scientific foundation models.

</details>


### [274] [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373)
*Longde Huang,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机权重平均（SWA）作为数据增强训练中深度集成的低成本替代方案，通过Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练轨迹，证明在无限宽度极限下SWA在增强数据上能提供超出单独SWA性能提升的等变性增益，并通过大量数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强是将学习任务的对称性融入神经网络的有效方式，但深度集成需要重复训练多次，成本高昂。作者希望寻找一种无需重复训练即可获得对称性（等变性）提升的替代方法。

Method: 将训练末期的随机训练轨迹近似为Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，在无限宽度极限下分析随机权重平均（SWA）对增强数据训练的对称性影响，并通过计算机视觉和图分类等多种模型（涵盖离散与连续对称性）进行数值验证。

Result: 理论分析表明，SWA在增强数据上提供了超出单独SWA性能提升所预期程度的等变性增强；大量数值实验验证了该结论的普适性。

Conclusion: SWA是一种有效的低成本替代方案，可在不重复训练的情况下获得类似于深度集成的对称性（等变性）收益，为在实际应用中高效利用数据增强的对称性提供了理论依据和实用方法。

Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical experiments on numerous models spanning computer vision and graph classification with both discrete and continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [275] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeaMoE架构，通过分组共享参数（部门内专家共享大部分参数+少量私有参数）和两阶段路由策略，解决MoE模型小批量解码时专家权重加载的显存带宽瓶颈，减少高达50.9%的每步加载权重，实现最高1.33倍端到端TPOT加速和2.00倍峰值加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型广泛用于实时交互应用（如编码助手、实时音视频交互），需要极低响应延迟，因此常采用小批量解码。但小批量下MoE推理变为内存受限，严重受限于专家权重加载（expert weight loading）。现有方案（后训练权重压缩、预训练阶段细粒度专家设计）要么损害模型精度，要么引入额外的计算和通信开销。该瓶颈尚未得到充分关注。

Method: DeaMoE是一种解码高效的MoE架构：(1) 将专家分组为多个department（部门），同一部门的专家来自相同专业领域，共享大部分参数以降低加载量，每个专家保留少量私有参数体现其独特性；(2) 设计定制化的两阶段路由策略（two-stage routing），避免冗余加载，提升LLM解码效率。

Result: 与vanilla MoE相比，DeaMoE将每步加载权重减少最高50.9%；在A40上对预训练7B模型实现最高1.33倍端到端TPOT加速；在微基准测试中，对DeepSeek-V3在A40和H100上分别实现最高2.00倍和1.97倍峰值加速。

Conclusion: DeaMoE通过部门级参数共享与两阶段路由策略有效解决了小批量解码场景下MoE的内存带宽瓶颈，在不牺牲精度的前提下显著提升解码效率，为实时交互场景下的MoE推理提供了新方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [276] [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414)
*Syed Abdul Haseeb Qadri,Bjarne C. Hiller,Felix Blanke,Vanja Sophie Cangalovic,Kutalmış Coşkun,Amin Mirzaei,Tom Siegl,Sebastian Bader,Thomas Kirste,Martin Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CytoBERT是一个面向单细胞流式细胞术数据的开源基础模型，通过自监督预训练于50M+细胞和15个人类数据集（含异构标记面板）上，实现了跨研究的细胞标记间关系学习，微调后可进行样本级分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式细胞术数据因实验方案和所选特征不同而高度异质且缺乏标准化，导致机器学习方法难以应用和跨研究迁移。尽管基础模型在诸多领域进展显著，但在此领域仍非常稀缺，亟需一个通用、可迁移的细胞术数据基础模型。

Method: 提出CytoBERT基础模型：(1) 通过标记标准化（marker standardization）对15个异构人类数据集（超过5000万细胞）进行整理，构建大规模细胞术语料； (2) 以自监督方式预训练，学习细胞内部可迁移的标记间关系；(3) 通过微调支持样本级分类任务。

Result: 微调后的CytoBERT在样本级分类任务中表现出色，证明了跨异构细胞术数据集进行迁移学习是可行的，为可扩展、可泛化的细胞术分析提供了起点。

Conclusion: CytoBERT证明了基础模型范式可成功应用于异构的流式细胞术数据，为领域内的迁移学习和标准化分析提供了可行的公开方案，有望推动免疫学研究和临床应用的规模化分析。

Abstract: Cytometry measures the complex characteristics of single cells (e.g., counts and protein expression of immune cells) and is widely used across immunological research and clinical settings. However, cytometry data is highly heterogeneous and unstandardized due to experimental protocols and the choice of measured features. While machine learning methods hold the potential to gain deeper insights into cell biology, these challenges make them difficult to apply and transfer across studies. Recent advances in foundation models can alleviate these issues, but corresponding approaches are still scarce in this field. To address this, we provide CytoBERT, a publicly available, open-source, open-weight foundation model for single-cell cytometry data with variable marker panels. CytoBERT is pretrained in a self-supervised manner on a large-scale cytometry corpus (15 human datasets with heterogeneous marker panels and more than 50 million cells) curated through marker standardization, enabling it to learn transferable inter-marker relationships within cells. Fine-tuning CytoBERT for sample-level classification demonstrates that transfer learning across heterogeneous cytometry datasets is feasible, providing a starting point for scalable, generalizable cytometry analysis. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [277] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Power Sampling在推理时采样中存在一个悖论：它能将更多概率质量推向正确轨迹，却反而使下游推理性能下降（自一致性场景下准确率最多下降18.5个百分点）。作者将该悖论归因于"剂量不匹配"和"覆盖不匹配"两个问题，并提出了一种变形控制、保支持的Power目标（deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target）来校准锐化程度并保留中等概率路径，最终在相同预算下逆转性能下降并超越标准多采样推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling作为一种免验证器的推理时改进方法，理论上应能锐化分布以提升推理质量，同时可作为各类下游采样方法的通用前端。但实际中却发现其会损害本应增强的下游推理，这一矛盾现象缺乏系统性解释，亟需诊断根源并提出修复方案。

Method: 作者首先通过自一致性作为代表性下游任务实证量化了Power Sampling导致的性能退化。然后诊断出两大不匹配：(1) 剂量不匹配——固定指数在不同问题上导致程度差异巨大的分布变化；(2) 覆盖不匹配——全局锐化将质量集中在少数主导路径上，尽管pass@k保持高值掩盖了多样性丧失，但支撑下游聚合、搜索和选择所需的广泛推理路径已丢失。基于此诊断，作者提出用变形控制、保支持的Power目标替代均匀轨迹幂运算，在跨问题间校准锐化强度，同时限制对中等概率路径的过度抑制。

Result: 在相同推理预算下，修复后的采样器与加权自一致性结合时，逆转了全局Power Sampling造成的性能损失，并在多个推理基准上优于标准多采样推理。原始Power Sampling导致自一致性准确率最多下降18.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 全局的、均匀的轨迹幂运算（Power Sampling）虽然能够增加正确路径的概率质量，却因剂量不匹配和覆盖不匹配而损害下游推理。通过引入变形控制和保支持目标的修复方案，能够在保持锐化收益的同时保留推理路径多样性，从而在受限预算下实现更优的推理性能。这提示了对推理时采样方法进行校准设计的重要性，而非简单依赖全局分布锐化。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [278] [Designing Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Models: A Unified Path-Space View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14430)
*Yixian Xu,Yuanrui Zhang,Shengjie Luo,Liwei Wang,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从路径空间原理出发，将现有扩散模型RL后训练中看似不同的两类算法（反向轨迹法和前向匹配法）统一到一个框架下，并通过重要性采样推导出策略梯度估计器，揭示了二者差异本质上是方差缩减效应而非RL原理分歧。在此基础上提出多样本KDE值梯度估计器和有界权重族，在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的RL对齐方法目前呈碎片化状态：反向轨迹方法依赖离散化似然比，前向匹配方法在带奖励标签的加噪版本上进行训练，两类方法缺乏统一的理论框架，难以系统理解和改进。

Method: 从正则化扩散RL目标出发，在采样SDE之间执行重要性采样以获得显式的轨迹空间策略梯度估计器。该估计器包含Flow-GRPO型更新所依赖的Itô随机积分，并推导出等价的方差缩减值梯度形式，可恢复AWM和DiffusionNFT的前向匹配结构。由此构建统一设计空间，涵盖值梯度估计、权重函数和采样选择三个维度，并提出多样本KDE值梯度估计器与有界权重族。

Result: 在SD3.5-M和Qwen-Image模型上的实验验证了方差缩减解释，所提出的方法在扩散RL基线之上取得了改进。

Conclusion: 扩散RL中反向轨迹与前向匹配两类方法的经验差距源于方差缩减效应而非RL原理分歧；统一框架下的新估计器和权重族可提升扩散模型对齐性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training provides a direct way to align diffusion models with human preferences and task-specific rewards. However, current RL algorithms for diffusion models remain fragmented: reverse-trajectory methods rely on discretized likelihood ratios, whereas forward-matching methods train on reward-labeled noising versions of the rollout samples. This paper shows that these seemingly different losses arise from a single path-space principle. Starting from the regularized diffusion-RL objective, we use importance sampling between sampling SDEs to obtain an explicit policy-gradient estimator on trajectory space. The estimator contains the stochastic Itô integral underlying Flow-GRPO-type updates; we derive an equivalent variance-reduced value-gradient form that recovers the forward-matching structure of AWM and DiffusionNFT. This identifies the empirical gap between these method families as a variance-reduction effect rather than a difference in RL principle. The derivation yields a unified design space organized by value-gradient estimation, weight functions, and sampling choices. Within this space, we propose a multi-sample KDE value-gradient estimator that reuses rollout groups, together with scale-bounded weight families that retain stable existing recipes while excluding singular ones. Experiments on SD3.5-M and Qwen-Image models validate the variance-reduction explanation and show that the resulting recipe improves over prior diffusion-RL baselines.

</details>


### [279] [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用的双层优化框架用于神经架构搜索（NAS），通过三种连续松弛公式（NAS-NG、NAS-MA、NAS-NGMA）将离散的神经元和激活层选择转化为可微优化问题，适用于MLP、CNN、RNN和Transformer等多种架构，实验表明能识别紧凑且预测性能强的架构，并在MNIST和CIFAR-10上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS天然被表述为双层优化问题，但由于离散的架构决策、指数增长的搜索空间以及训练候选架构的高成本，计算开销极大。需要一种可扩展的通用NAS框架，能够在不牺牲性能的情况下高效搜索紧凑架构。

Method: 提出三种可扩展的公式化方法，将离散的神经元级和激活级决策替换为连续松弛，实现组合架构空间的可微优化。包括：基于神经元门控的NAS（NAS-NG）、基于混合激活的NAS（NAS-MA）、以及两者结合的NAS（NAS-NGMA）。

Result: 在MNIST上，NAS-NGMA以7.69M MLP参数达到98.68%测试精度，NAS-NG以仅0.26M CNN参数达到99.63%精度。在CIFAR-10上，所提方法一致优于vanilla DARTS。NAS-NG还能优化过参数化和文献最优架构，在减少参数的同时提高精度。

Conclusion: 松弛双层优化可作为离散NAS的可扩展替代方案，为高效的神经元级和激活级架构优化提供了通用框架，适用于多种架构类型。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is naturally formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper-level optimizes the architecture using validation performance and the lower-level trains network parameters using training loss. However, NAS is computationally expensive due to discrete architectural decisions, exponentially growing search spaces, and the high cost of training candidate architectures. This work develops a general bilevel optimization framework for NAS across diverse architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, to identify compact architectures with strong predictive performance. We propose three scalable formulations that replace discrete neuron- and activation-level decisions with continuous relaxations, enabling differentiable optimization over otherwise combinatorial architecture spaces. These formulations give rise to three NAS methods: NAS based on Neuron Gating (NAS-NG), NAS based on Mixed Activation (NAS-MA), and NAS based on Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation (NAS-NGMA). Experiments on MLPs and CNNs using MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that the proposed methods consistently identify compact architectures with competitive or improved predictive performance. On MNIST, NAS-NGMA achieves 98.68% test accuracy with 7.69M MLP parameters, while NAS-NG achieves 99.63% accuracy with only 0.26M CNN parameters. On CIFAR-10, the proposed methods consistently outperform vanilla DARTS. Further experiments demonstrate that NAS-NG can optimize substantially over-parameterized and literature-optimal architectures, improving accuracy while reducing parameters. These results establish relaxed bilevel optimization as a scalable alternative to discrete NAS and provide a general framework for efficient neuron- and activation-level architecture optimization.

</details>


### [280] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-NAS，一个基于线性规划的微分神经架构搜索框架。LP-NAS利用验证损失梯度和训练损失海森矩阵构建线性规划，计算既能提升泛化性能又保持模型参数最优性的架构更新方向。通过两种高效变体S-LP-NAS和R-LP-NAS，在DARTS搜索空间上实现S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS算法，在CIFAR-10/100上超越标准DARTS，并与P-DARTS、PC-DARTS、STO-DARTS等变体对比，同时在ImageNet上验证了架构迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分NAS将架构搜索空间松弛为连续域，因此可以运用连续优化原理。现有方法未充分利用数学模型化框架来同时保证架构优化的泛化性和模型参数最优性，需要一个系统化的数学规划框架来更高效地引导架构搜索。

Method: LP-NAS将架构搜索问题建模为线性规划（LP），利用验证损失梯度保持泛化目标，利用训练损失Hessian矩阵约束模型参数最优性，构建可求解的LP来计算架构更新方向。引入两种计算高效变体：S-LP-NAS（标准的LP求解）和R-LP-NAS（简化/近似解法），并分别应用于DARTS搜索空间得到S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS算法。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，LP-DARTS在架构搜索和评估阶段均优于标准DARTS，早期搜索迭代中收敛更快且验证性能显著更高。与P-DARTS、PC-DARTS、STO-DARTS等变体对比也表现出有效性，并在ImageNet上验证了所发现架构的迁移能力。

Conclusion: LP-NAS证明了数学规划框架在微分NAS中的有效性，通过线性规划引导架构搜索方向，能够同时兼顾泛化性能与模型参数最优性，实现更快收敛和更高验证精度，且生成架构具备良好的跨数据集迁移性。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [281] [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492)
*Ben Anson,Conor Houghton,Edward Milsom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出sMuon方法，通过在低秩设定下线性化并利用最小二乘近似松弛的Muon目标，解决了Muon优化器无法与LoRA自然结合的问题（因为低秩参数化无法对权重更新进行正交化）。sMuon仅使用矩阵乘法运算实现高效求解，在SFT和ReLoRA预训练实验中表现良好，总体上为低秩微调带来适度的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在神经网络预训练中相比其他优化器具有明显优势，但在参数高效微调（PEFT）中使用较少。潜在原因是最常见PEFT方法LoRA与Muon无法自然结合——低秩参数化下无法对权重更新进行正交化，这阻碍了Muon在微调场景中的应用。

Method: 通过线性化将松弛的Muon目标目标在低秩设定下进行近似，然后利用最小二乘方法求解。实现上仅使用矩阵乘法（matmul）运算，避免了复杂的线性代数分解例程，从而保证了计算效率。基于此方法提出sMuon（small Muon）优化器。

Result: sMuon在SFT和有ReLoRA参与的预训练实验中表现良好。虽然结果依模型和评估基准而异，但总体上将Muon应用于低秩微调相较于基线提供了适度的性能改进。

Conclusion: sMuon成功将Muon优化器扩展到低秩微调场景，弥补了LoRA与Muon之间无法数学结合的鸿沟。尽管改进幅度为中等水平，该方法为在PEFT中使用Muon类优化器提供了可行且高效的途径。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer shows clear benefits versus alternatives when pretraining neural networks. However, it is used less frequently for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. We provide an efficient implementation that uses matmul operations only, as opposed to more complex linear algebra decomposition routines. Our method, sMuon (small Muon), performs favourably across SFT and a ReLoRA pretraining experiment. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

</details>


### [282] [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496)
*Hao Yan,Lisa Pilgram,Dan Liu,Linglong Kong,Fida Dankar,Khaled El Emam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段跨表格数据生成（CTDG）框架，将异构原始表格转换为标准统计表格，再使用扩散Transformer模型生成合成统计表格，最后通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建原始表格式的合成数据，支持从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型并生成任意数量的逼真合成表格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成表格数据生成方法主要局限于单输入表格场景，难以有效处理具有不同特征集的多个异构表格。作者旨在解决跨表格数据生成的挑战，使生成模型能够从多个异构表格中学习并生成新的合成表格数据。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段将每个异构原始表格转换为具有统一列集合的标准统计表格，捕获各原始列的边际分布和列间成对相关性；第二阶段使用扩散Transformer模型在同质统计表格上训练，学习结构模式并生成合成统计表格，随后通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换从统计表格重建合成原始表格。

Result: 实验结果表明所提出的方法在学习统计表示上具有高保真度，且在合成数据的保真度-多样性权衡上表现良好，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段CTDG框架实现了从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型的能力，并能生成无限数量的逼真合成异构表格，在保真度和多样性之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Cross-Tabular Data Generation (CTDG) seeks to learn a generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and produce new synthetic tabular datasets. However, existing synthetic tabular data generation methods are largely restricted to single-input-table scenarios and struggle to effectively handle multiple heterogeneous tables with diverse feature sets. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for cross-tabular data generation. In the first stage, each heterogeneous raw table is transformed into a standardized statistical table with the same set of columns across all tables. Each statistical table captures the marginal distributions of the original columns and the pairwise correlations among them. In the second stage, a diffusion transformer model is trained to capture structural patterns across these homogeneous statistical tables and to generate synthetic statistical tables. Synthetic raw tables are subsequently reconstructed from the generated statistical tables via multivariate Gaussian sampling followed by an inverse probability integral transform. This two-stage CTDG framework enables the learning of a unified generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and supports the generation of an unlimited number of realistic synthetic heterogeneous tables. Experimental results demonstrate high fidelity in the learned statistical representations and a favorable fidelity-diversity trade-off in the generated synthetic data, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [283] [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)
*Hanfeng Lu,Tianyu Feng,Suyi Li,Yuheng Zhao,Wei Gao,Shaopan Xiong,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Rollplex是一个针对视觉语言模型(VLM)在线强化学习(RL)后训练的运行时系统，通过将参考计算和训练阶段的prefix处理移入rollout解码窗口，实现prefix计算与rollout解码的并发执行，在32块H800 GPU上相比串行colocation提升1.23x-1.30x速度，相比disaggregation方案提升1.57x-2.24x，同时保持同步on-policy RL更新语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的on-policy RL运行时将rollout、reference scoring和actor training以严格串行阶段执行，这对文本RL有效，但对VLM而言浪费严重——因为VLM处理密集视频输入和prompt前缀占据每个阶段很大比例的计算量。由于prefix处理与生成响应无关，可以在rollout解码期间并行执行，而当前run-time导致GPU算力利用不足。

Method: Rollplex提出两个核心机制：(1) 阶段感知内存管理(Phase-aware memory management)——根据生产者-消费者生命周期控制HBM驻留，在rollout解码窗口内调度prefix计算；(2) 并行度感知权重共享(Parallelism-aware weight sharing)——对不同TP度数下布局兼容的张量共享同一物理存储，仅重建不兼容的张量，避免完整的第二份actor副本。这解决了Qwen2.5-VL-32B朴素colocation需约165 GiB/GPU以及rollout和training偏好不同TP度和权重布局的问题。

Result: 在32块H800 GPU上，Rollplex相比串行colocation实现1.23x-1.30x加速，相比相同GPU预算下的disaggregation方案实现1.57x-2.24x加速，同时保持同步RL更新的语义不变。

Conclusion: 通过将prefix计算与rollout解码重叠，并利用阶段感知内存管理和并行度感知权重共享来克服内存与布局约束，Rollplex能够在保持同步on-policy RL语义的同时显著提升VLM RL后训练的效率。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training enhances these capabilities using task feedback, but current on-policy RL runtimes execute rollout, reference scoring, and actor training in strict serial phases. While effective for text-only RL, this phase-granular execution is wasteful for VLMs, where processing dense video inputs and prompt prefixes occupies a large fraction of each phase. Because prefix processing is independent of the generated response, it can be run alongside rollout decoding, which leaves GPU compute capacity underutilized, without breaking synchronous on-policy semantics.
  We present Rollplex, a runtime that decomposes the reference and training phase and moves the prefix computation into the rollout decode window. Realizing this schedule requires more than concurrent kernel launches: naive colocation of Qwen2.5-VL-32\,B requires roughly 165\,GiB per GPU, while rollout and training prefer different tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and weight layouts. Rollplex addresses these constraints with two mechanisms. Phase-aware memory management controls HBM residency according to producer--consumer lifetimes. Parallelism-aware weight sharing uses the same physical storage for layout-compatible tensors across distinct TP degrees and reconstructs only incompatible tensors, avoiding a complete second actor copy. On 32 H800 GPUs, Rollplex achieves $1.23\times$--$1.30\times$ speedup over serial colocation and $1.57\times$--$2.24\times$ over disaggregation under the same GPU budget, while preserving the synchronous RL update.

</details>


### [284] [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)
*Pin-Yen Huang,Sachin Chhabra,Prasanth Sai Gouripeddi,Abhinav Kumar,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RecipeNet是一种层级Transformer架构，用于配方数据的表示学习，通过编码步骤内字段交互和步骤间序列依赖，在多个配方数据集和任务上优于现有表格学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配方数据（如材料合成、药物配方、工业制造）以有序步骤序列和异构结构化字段表示，但现有表格学习方法将结构扁平化为固定模式表示，无法捕捉层级字段交互和过程依赖关系。

Method: RecipeNet采用层级Transformer架构，通过堆叠的Transformer编码器分别在两个层级进行建模：(1) 步骤内的字段级交互编码；(2) 步骤间的序列依赖编码。

Result: 在多个配方数据集和任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet一致性地优于现有表格模型。

Conclusion: 层级与序列建模对配方表示学习具有重要价值，RecipeNet为此类结构化过程数据提供了有效的建模方案。

Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.

</details>
